@dataclass
class InsuranceMaster:
    INS_LIFE: bool = False
    INS_HEALTH: bool = False
    INS_AUTO: bool = False
    INS_HOME: bool = False
    INS_UMBRELLA: bool = False
    INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    INS_CLAIMS_COUNT: int = 0
    INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class InvestmentMaster:
    INV_QUANTITY: int = 0
    INV_CURRENT_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

main_data = MainData()

insurance_master = InsuranceMaster()

investment_master = InvestmentMaster()

transaction_log = TransactionLog()

customer_master = CustomerMaster()

account_master = AccountMaster()

loan_master = LoanMaster()

def is_number(s: str) -> bool:
    try:
        float(s)
        return True
    except ValueError:
        return False

def check_credit_C2(ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal, ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT: Decimal) -> None:
    """
           ELSE
               SET ws_found TO TRUE
           end_if.
    """
    global main_data
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT:
        main_data.WS_FOUND = False
    else:
        main_data.WS_FOUND = True

    renew_policies_C2()

    """
    print("PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES...")

def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """
       4200-calculate_premiums.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
           SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
               READ insurance_master NEXT
                   AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM 4210-determine_base_premium
                       PERFORM 4220-apply_risk_factor
                       PERFORM 4230-calculate_final_premium
               end_read
           end_perform.
    """
    global main_data, insurance_master
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    main_data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not main_data.WS_EOF:
        #Simulating read insurance_master NEXT
        #assuming a list of InsuranceMaster records
        insurance_master_list = [InsuranceMaster(), InsuranceMaster(), InsuranceMaster()]
        if insurance_master_list:
            insurance_master = insurance_master_list.pop(0)
            determine_base_premium_C2()
            apply_risk_factor_C2()
            calculate_final_premium_C2()
        else:
            main_data.WS_EOF = True

def determine_base_premium_C2() -> None:
    """
       4210-determine_base_premium.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN ins_life
                   COMPUTE ws_calc_amount = 
                       ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_life_rate_per_1000
               WHEN ins_health
                   MOVE ws_health_base_premium TO ws_calc_amount
               WHEN ins_auto
                   MOVE ws_auto_base_premium TO ws_calc_amount
               WHEN ins_home
                   COMPUTE ws_calc_amount = 
                       ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_home_rate_per_1000
               WHEN ins_umbrella
                   MOVE ws_umbrella_rate TO ws_calc_amount
           end_evaluate.
    """
    global main_data, insurance_master
    match True:
        case insurance_master.INS_LIFE:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * main_data.WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case insurance_master.INS_HEALTH:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case insurance_master.INS_AUTO:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case insurance_master.INS_HOME:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * main_data.WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case insurance_master.INS_UMBRELLA:
            main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def apply_risk_factor_C2() -> None:
    """
       4220-apply_risk_factor.
           IF ins_claims_count > 2
               COMPUTE ws_calc_amount = ws_calc_amount * 1.25
           end_if.
    """
    global main_data, insurance_master
    if insurance_master.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
        main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2() -> None:
    """
       4230-calculate_final_premium.
           MOVE ws_calc_amount TO ins_premium_amount
           ADD ws_calc_amount TO ws_total_premiums.
    """
    global main_data, insurance_master
    insurance_master.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    main_data.WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    """
       5200-calculate_portfolio_value.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES..."
           SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
               READ investment_master NEXT
                   AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END

                       PERFORM 5220-calculate_gain_loss
                       PERFORM 5230-update_totals
               end_read

    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    main_data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not main_data.WS_EOF:

        investment_master_list = [InvestmentMaster(), InvestmentMaster(), InvestmentMaster()]
        if investment_master_list:
            investment_master = investment_master_list.pop(0)
            calculate_position_value_C2()
            calculate_gain_loss_C2()
            update_totals_C2()
        else:
            main_data.WS_EOF = True

def calculate_position_value_C2() -> None:
    """
       5210-calculate_position_value.
           COMPUTE inv_market_value = 
               inv_quantity * inv_current_price.
    """
    global main_data, investment_master
    investment_master.INV_MARKET_VALUE = Decimal(investment_master.INV_QUANTITY) * investment_master.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

def calculate_gain_loss_C2() -> None:
    """
       5220-calculate_gain_loss.
           COMPUTE inv_gain_loss = 
               inv_market_value - (inv_quantity * inv_purchase_price).
    """
    global main_data, investment_master
    investment_master.INV_GAIN_LOSS = investment_master.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (Decimal(investment_master.INV_QUANTITY) * investment_master.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

def update_totals_C2() -> None:
    """
       5230-update_totals.
           ADD inv_market_value TO ws_total_investments.
    """
    global main_data, investment_master
    main_data.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_master.INV_MARKET_VALUE

def calculate_dividends_C2() -> None:
    """
       5400-calculate_dividends.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
           SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
               READ investment_master NEXT
                   AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       IF inv_dividend_rate > 0
                           PERFORM 5410-compute_dividend
                           PERFORM 5420-post_dividend
                       end_if
               end_read
           end_perform.
    """
    global main_data, investment_master
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    main_data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not main_data.WS_EOF:
        #Simulating READ investment_master NEXT
        investment_master_list = [InvestmentMaster(INV_DIVIDEND_RATE = Decimal("0.05")), InvestmentMaster(), InvestmentMaster()]
        if investment_master_list:
            investment_master = investment_master_list.pop(0)
            if investment_master.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                compute_dividend_C2()
                post_dividend_C2()
        else:
            main_data.WS_EOF = True

def compute_dividend_C2() -> None:
    """
       5410-compute_dividend.
           COMPUTE ws_calc_amount = 
               inv_market_value * inv_dividend_rate / 4.
    """
    global main_data, investment_master
    main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = investment_master.INV_MARKET_VALUE * investment_master.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE / 4

def post_dividend_C2() -> None:
    """
       5420-post_dividend.
           ADD ws_calc_amount TO ws_total_dividends.
    """
    global main_data
    main_data.WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS += main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def daily_summary_C2() -> None:
    """
       6100-daily_summary.
           DISPLAY "GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY..."
           MOVE SPACES TO report_line
           STRING "mega_enterprise DAILY SUMMARY - " DELIMITED SIZE
                  ws_current_date DELIMITED SIZE
                  INTO report_line
           WRITE report_line
           PERFORM 6110-write_totals.
    """
    global main_data
    print("GENERATING DAILY SUMMARY...")
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = ""
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = "mega_enterprise DAILY SUMMARY - " + main_data.WS_CURRENT_DATE
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)
    write_totals_C2()

def write_totals_C2() -> None:
    """
       6110-write_totals.
           MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ws_formatted_amount
           STRING "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " DELIMITED SIZE
                  ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
                  INTO report_line
           WRITE report_line
           
           MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ws_formatted_amount
           STRING "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " DELIMITED SIZE
                  ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
                  INTO report_line
           WRITE report_line
           
           MOVE ws_total_loans TO ws_formatted_amount
           STRING "TOTAL LOANS: " DELIMITED SIZE
                  ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
                  INTO report_line
           WRITE report_line.
    """
    global main_data
    main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(main_data.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS)
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " + main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)

    main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(main_data.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " + main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)

    main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(main_data.WS_TOTAL_LOANS)
    main_data.REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL LOANS: " + main_data.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    print(main_data.REPORT_LINE)

def write_transaction_C2() -> None:
    """
       8100-write_transaction.
           MOVE ws_current_timestamp TO tran_timestamp
           MOVE 'DEP' TO tran_type
           MOVE ws_calc_amount TO tran_amount
           MOVE 'C' TO tran_status
           WRITE transaction_record.
    """

    TRAN_TYPE = 'DEP'
    TRAN_AMOUNT = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    TRAN_STATUS = 'C'
    print(f"Writing transaction: Timestamp={TRAN_TIMESTAMP}, Type={TRAN_TYPE}, Amount={TRAN_AMOUNT}, Status={TRAN_STATUS}") # simulating write

def format_date_C2() -> None:
    """
       8300-format_date.
           STRING ws_temp_date(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE
                  ws_temp_date(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE
                  ws_temp_date(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE
                  INTO ws_formatted_date.
    """
    global main_data
    main_data.WS_FORMATTED_DATE = f"{main_data.WS_TEMP_DATE[0:4]}-{main_data.WS_TEMP_DATE[4:6]}-{main_data.WS_TEMP_DATE[6:8]}"

def validate_account_C2() -> None:
    """
       8400-validate_account.
           SET ws_valid TO TRUE
           IF acct_id = SPACES
               SET ws_invalid TO TRUE
           end_if.
    """
    global main_data
    main_data.WS_VALID = True
    if main_data.ACCT_ID == "":
        main_data.WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """
       8500-calculate_tax.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_1_max
                   COMPUTE ws_calc_tax = 
                       ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_1_rate
               WHEN ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_2_max
                   COMPUTE ws_calc_tax = 
                       (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) +
                       ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_1_max) * 
                        ws_bracket_2_rate)
               WHEN ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_3_max
                   COMPUTE ws_calc_tax = 
                       (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) +
                       ((ws_bracket_2_max - ws_bracket_1_max) * 
                        ws_bracket_2_rate) +
                       ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_2_max) * 
                        ws_bracket_3_rate)
               WHEN OTHER
                   COMPUTE ws_calc_tax = 
                       ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_5_rate
           end_evaluate.
    """
    global main_data
    match True:
        case main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = (main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                    ((main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= main_data.WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = (main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                    ((main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - main_data.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                                    ((main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - main_data.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * main_data.WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            main_data.WS_CALC_TAX = main_data.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * main_data.WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:

ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

WS_ANNUAL_FEE_CARD: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """9310-customer_segmentation."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading customer_master record
        CUSTOMER_MASTER = CustomerMaster()  # Replace with actual data loading
        
        if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """9311-calculate_clv."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_PERSONAL_RATE
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                         (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                         (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """9312-assign_segment."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """9632-sweep_accounts."""
    global ACCT_BALANCE, ACCT_MIN_BALANCE, WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT_BALANCE -= WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading customer_master record
        CUSTOMER_MASTER = CustomerMaster()  # Replace with actual data loading
        
        if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

    """A121-cleanse_data."""

        WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

@dataclass
class TransactionLog:
    """Represents a transaction log record."""
    tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class LoanRecord:
    """Represents a loan record."""
    loan_current_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    loan_paid_off: bool = False

cust_status: str = ""
ws_temp_code: str = ""
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_error_count: int = 0
cust_ssn: str = ""
cust_credit_score: int = 0
cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
cust_risk_rating: str = ""
ws_current_timestamp: str = ""
ws_temp_string: str = ""
ws_valid: bool = False
ws_atm_fee_foreign: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_cust_count: int = 0
ws_formatted_count: str = ""

transaction_log: TransactionLog = TransactionLog()
loan_record: LoanRecord = LoanRecord()

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """C100-transaction_monitoring."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")  # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    ws_not_eof = True  # SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    while not ws_eof:  # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        # READ transaction_log NEXT
        #     AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
        #     NOT AT END
        #         PERFORM C110-rule_based_detection
        #         PERFORM C120-behavior_analysis
        #         PERFORM C130-network_analysis
        # end_read
        try:
            transaction_log = get_next_transaction()
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
            c130_network_analysis_C4()

        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof = True # AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            break

def get_next_transaction() -> TransactionLog:
    """Dummy function to simulate reading a transaction log."""
    # Replace this with actual file reading logic
    global transaction_log
    if get_next_transaction.counter < 3:  # simulate a few transactions
        get_next_transaction.counter += 1
        return TransactionLog(tran_amount = Decimal(get_next_transaction.counter * 4000))
    else:
        raise StopIteration # Simulate end of file
get_next_transaction.counter = 0


def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """C110-rule_based_detection."""
    if transaction_log.tran_amount >= 10000:  # IF tran_amount >= 10000
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()  # PERFORM C111-flag_ctr
    if transaction_log.tran_amount >= 5000 and transaction_log.tran_amount < 10000:  # IF tran_amount >= 5000 AND tran_amount < 10000
        c112_check_structuring_C4()  # PERFORM C112-check_structuring

def c111_flag_ctr_C4() -> None:
    """C111-flag_ctr."""
    global ws_process_count
    ws_process_count += 1  # ADD 1 TO ws_process_count.

def c112_check_structuring_C4() -> None:
    """C112-check_structuring."""
    global ws_error_count
    ws_error_count += 1  # ADD 1 TO ws_error_count.

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    global cust_risk_rating
    if cust_credit_score > 750:  # IF cust_credit_score > 750
        cust_risk_rating = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO cust_risk_rating
    elif cust_credit_score > 650:  # ELSE IF cust_credit_score > 650
        cust_risk_rating = 'B'  # MOVE 'B' TO cust_risk_rating
    elif cust_credit_score > 550:  # ELSE IF cust_credit_score > 550
        cust_risk_rating = 'C'  # MOVE 'C' TO cust_risk_rating
    else:  # ELSE
        cust_risk_rating = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO cust_risk_rating

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = (cust_credit_score * 10) + (cust_total_balance / 1000) - (cust_total_loans / 2000)  # COMPUTE ws_calc_result = (cust_credit_score * 10) + (cust_total_balance / 1000) - (cust_total_loans / 2000).

def main_loop() -> None:
    """
    Lines: 1-8
    """
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Assuming customer_master is a file-like object with a 'next' method
        try:
            customer = CUSTOMER_MASTER.next()  # type: ignore
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF = True
            break
        update_profile_I110_C5()
        enrich_profile_I120_C5()
        global WS_CUST_COUNT
        WS_CUST_COUNT += 1

def open_files_1100_C5() -> None:

    """

        ACCOUNT_FILE = open("account_file", "r")  # type: ignore

        REPORT_FILE = open("report_file", "w")  # type: ignore

        MASTER_FILE = open("master_file", "r+")  # type: ignore
        global WS_FILE_STATUS
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'
    except Exception as e:
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '99'
        global WS_ERROR_MSG
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'  # type: ignore
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def initialize_tables_1300_C5() -> None:
    """
    Lines: 189-201
    """
    global RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY, RT_RATE, RT_CODE
    RATE_TABLE_ENTRY = [None] * 100  # type: ignore
    BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY = [None] * 50  # type: ignore
    RT_RATE = [Decimal('0')] * 100 # type: ignore
    RT_CODE = [''] * 100 # type: ignore
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = None  # type: ignore
        RT_RATE[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = Decimal('0')
        RT_CODE[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = ''
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):
        BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = None  # type: ignore

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """
    Lines: 203-219
    """
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_REF_RECORD, RT_CODE, RT_RATE
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # type: ignore
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        try:
            line = REFERENCE_FILE.readline() # type: ignore
            if not line:
                WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # type: ignore
                break
            WS_REF_RECORD = line.strip()  # type: ignore
            WS_REF_CODE = WS_REF_RECORD[:10]  # type: ignore
            WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(WS_REF_RECORD[10:])  # type: ignore
            RT_CODE[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = WS_REF_CODE
            RT_RATE[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = WS_REF_RATE
            WS_TBL_IDX += 1
        except Exception as e:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # type: ignore
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # type: ignore

def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """
    Lines: 222-236
    """
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_TRANSACTION_REC
    try:
        line = TRANSACTION_FILE.readline()  # type: ignore
        if not line:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # type: ignore
            return
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC = line.strip()  # type: ignore
        WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1 # type: ignore
        validate_transaction_2100_C5()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # type: ignore
            process_by_type_2200_C5()
        else:
            handle_error_2900_C5()
    except Exception as e:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # type: ignore

def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """
    Lines: 238-257
    """
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'  # type: ignore
    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == '' or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID is None:  # type: ignore
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # type: ignore
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'  # type: ignore
        return
    try:
        float(TXN_AMOUNT) # type: ignore
    except ValueError:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # type: ignore
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'  # type: ignore
        return
    if TXN_TYPE not in ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']: # type: ignore
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # type: ignore
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'  # type: ignore
    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5() -> None:
    """
    Lines: 267-276
    """
    global TXN_TYPE, TXN_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG
    if TXN_TYPE == 'W':  # type: ignore
        if TXN_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:  # type: ignore
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # type: ignore
            WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # type: ignore
    if TXN_AMOUNT > 1000000:  # type: ignore
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # type: ignore
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'  # type: ignore

def process_by_type_2200_C5() -> None:
    """
    Lines: 278-286
    """

    match TXN_TYPE:  # type: ignore
        case 'D':
            process_deposit_2300_C5()
        case 'W':

        case 'T':
            process_transfer_2500_C5()
        case 'I':
            process_interest_2600_C5()
        case _:
            handle_error_2900_C5()

def unknown_C6(condition: str) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    WHEN OTHER
       MOVE 2.5 TO ws_interest_rate
    end_evaluate."""
    match condition:
        case _:
            global WS_INTEREST_RATE
            WS_INTEREST_RATE = Decimal('2.5')

def calculate_simple_interest_C6() -> None:
    """7200-calculate_simple_interest.
    COMPUTE ws_simple_interest = 
       ws_account_balance * ws_interest_rate 
       * ws_days_in_period / 36500."""
    global WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST
    WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * WS_INTEREST_RATE * WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD / Decimal('36500.00')

def calculate_compound_interest_C6() -> None:
    """7300-calculate_compound_interest.
    COMPUTE ws_compound_factor = 
       (1 + ws_interest_rate / 36500) 
       ** ws_days_in_period
    COMPUTE ws_compound_interest = 
       ws_account_balance * (ws_compound_factor - 1)."""
    global WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR, WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST
    WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + WS_INTEREST_RATE / Decimal('36500.00')) ** WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD
    WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * (WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)

def apply_interest_C6() -> None:
    """7400-apply_interest.
    IF ws_interest_method = 'S'
       ADD ws_simple_interest TO ws_account_balance
    ELSE
       ADD ws_compound_interest TO ws_account_balance
    end_if
    PERFORM 2350-update_account."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    if WS_INTEREST_METHOD == 'S':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST
    else:
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST
    update_account_C6()

def fee_processing_C6() -> None:
    """8000-fee_processing.
    PERFORM 8100-calculate_monthly_fee
    PERFORM 8200-calculate_transaction_fees
    PERFORM 8300-apply_fee_waivers
    PERFORM 8400-deduct_fees."""
    calculate_monthly_fee_C6()
    calculate_transaction_fees_C6()
    apply_fee_waivers_C6()
    deduct_fees_C6()

def calculate_monthly_fee_C6() -> None:
    """8100-calculate_monthly_fee.
    EVALUATE ws_account_type
       WHEN 'CHK'
          MOVE 12.00 TO ws_monthly_fee
       WHEN 'SAV'
          MOVE 5.00 TO ws_monthly_fee
       WHEN 'PRM'
          MOVE 25.00 TO ws_monthly_fee
       WHEN OTHER
          MOVE 0.00 TO ws_monthly_fee
    end_evaluate."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE
    match WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE:
        case 'CHK':
            WS_MONTHLY_FEE = Decimal('12.00')
        case 'SAV':
            WS_MONTHLY_FEE = Decimal('5.00')
        case 'PRM':
            WS_MONTHLY_FEE = Decimal('25.00')
        case _:
            WS_MONTHLY_FEE = Decimal('0.00')

def calculate_transaction_fees_C6() -> None:
    """8200-calculate_transaction_fees.
    IF ws_trans_count > ws_free_trans_limit
       COMPUTE ws_excess_trans = 
          ws_trans_count - ws_free_trans_limit
       COMPUTE ws_trans_fee = 
          ws_excess_trans * ws_per_trans_fee
    ELSE
       MOVE ZEROES TO ws_trans_fee
    end_if."""
    global WS_EXCESS_TRANS, WS_TRANS_FEE
    if WS_TRANS_COUNT > WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT:
        WS_EXCESS_TRANS = WS_TRANS_COUNT - WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT
        WS_TRANS_FEE = WS_EXCESS_TRANS * WS_PER_TRANS_FEE
    else:
        WS_TRANS_FEE = Decimal('0.00')

def apply_fee_waivers_C6() -> None:
    """8300-apply_fee_waivers.
    IF ws_account_balance >= ws_min_balance_waiver
       MOVE ZEROES TO ws_monthly_fee
    end_if
    IF ws_customer_tier = 'GOLD' OR 'PLATINUM'
       COMPUTE ws_trans_fee = ws_trans_fee * 0.5
    end_if."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE, WS_TRANS_FEE
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= WS_MIN_BALANCE_WAIVER:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE = Decimal('0.00')
    if WS_CUSTOMER_TIER == 'GOLD' or WS_CUSTOMER_TIER == 'PLATINUM':
        WS_TRANS_FEE = WS_TRANS_FEE * Decimal('0.5')

def deduct_fees_C6() -> None:
    """8400-deduct_fees.
    COMPUTE ws_total_fees = 
       ws_monthly_fee + ws_trans_fee
    SUBTRACT ws_total_fees FROM ws_account_balance
    PERFORM 2350-update_account
    PERFORM 8450-record_fee_transaction."""
    global WS_TOTAL_FEES, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_TOTAL_FEES = WS_MONTHLY_FEE + WS_TRANS_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_TOTAL_FEES
    update_account_C6()
    record_fee_transaction_C6()

def record_fee_transaction_C6() -> None:
    """8450-record_fee_transaction.
    INITIALIZE ws_fee_record
    MOVE txn_account_id TO fee_account
    MOVE ws_total_fees TO fee_amount
    MOVE 'MONTHLY FEE' TO fee_description
    MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO fee_date
    WRITE fee_record FROM ws_fee_record."""
    global WS_FEE_RECORD
    WS_FEE_RECORD = WsFeeRecord()
    WS_FEE_RECORD.FEE_ACCOUNT = TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    WS_FEE_RECORD.FEE_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_FEE_RECORD.FEE_DESCRIPTION = 'MONTHLY FEE'
    WS_FEE_RECORD.FEE_DATE = str(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))
    write_fee_record_C6(WS_FEE_RECORD)

def write_fee_record_C6(fee_record: WsFeeRecord) -> None:
    # Dummy function for writing fee record
    print(f"Writing fee record: {fee_record}")
    pass

def finalization_C6() -> None:
    """9000-FINALIZATION.
    PERFORM 9100-write_control_totals
    PERFORM 9200-close_files
    PERFORM 9300-display_summary."""
    write_control_totals_C6()
    close_files_C6()
    display_summary_C6()

def write_control_totals_C6() -> None:
    """9100-write_control_totals.
    INITIALIZE ws_control_record
    MOVE ws_trans_count TO ctl_trans_count
    MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ctl_deposits
    MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ctl_withdrawals
    MOVE ws_error_count TO ctl_error_count
    MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ctl_run_date
    WRITE control_record FROM ws_control_record."""
    global WS_CONTROL_RECORD
    WS_CONTROL_RECORD = WsControlRecord()
    WS_CONTROL_RECORD.CTL_TRANS_COUNT = WS_TRANS_COUNT
    WS_CONTROL_RECORD.CTL_DEPOSITS = WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS
    WS_CONTROL_RECORD.CTL_WITHDRAWALS = WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS
    WS_CONTROL_RECORD.CTL_ERROR_COUNT = WS_ERROR_COUNT
    WS_CONTROL_RECORD.CTL_RUN_DATE = str(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))
    write_control_record_C6(WS_CONTROL_RECORD)

def write_control_record_C6(control_record: WsControlRecord) -> None:
    # Dummy function for writing control record
    print(f"Writing control record: {control_record}")
    pass

def display_summary_C6() -> None:
    """9300-display_summary.
    DISPLAY '=========================================='
    DISPLAY 'mega_enterprise PROCESSING COMPLETE'
    DISPLAY '=========================================='
    DISPLAY 'TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: ' ws_trans_count
    DISPLAY 'DEPOSITS:              ' ws_deposit_count

def abort_process_C6() -> None:
    """9500-abort_process.
    DISPLAY 'CRITICAL ERROR: ' ws_abort_reason
    DISPLAY 'PROCESSING ABORTED AT ' 
       FUNCTION current_date
    PERFORM 9200-close_files
    STOP RUN WITH STATUS 8."""

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms) -> None:
    """10335-calculate_pmi."""
    match True:
        case ws.ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
            ws.ws_pmi_amount = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12  # COMPUTE ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * 0.0125 / 12
        case ws.ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
            ws.ws_pmi_amount = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12  # COMPUTE ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * 0.0100 / 12
        case ws.ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
            ws.ws_pmi_amount = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12  # COMPUTE ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * 0.0075 / 12
        case _:
            ws.ws_pmi_amount = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12  # COMPUTE ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * 0.0050 / 12

def evaluate_history_C7(ws: WorkingStorage) -> None:
    """10340-evaluate_history."""
    if ws.ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws.ws_risk_score -= 50  # SUBTRACT 50 FROM ws_risk_score
        ws.ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'  # MOVE 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY' TO ws_factor_1
    if ws.ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws.ws_risk_score -= 30  # SUBTRACT 30 FROM ws_risk_score
        ws.ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'  # MOVE '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES' TO ws_factor_2
    if ws.ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws.ws_risk_score -= 20  # SUBTRACT 20 FROM ws_risk_score
        ws.ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'  # MOVE 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES' TO ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws: WorkingStorage) -> None:
    """10350-calculate_final_risk."""
    ws.ws_risk_score = ws.ws_risk_score / 4  # COMPUTE ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    match True:
        case ws.ws_risk_score >= 80:
            ws.ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'  # MOVE 'LOW RISK' TO ws_risk_category
        case ws.ws_risk_score >= 60:
            ws.ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'  # MOVE 'MODERATE' TO ws_risk_category
        case ws.ws_risk_score >= 40:
            ws.ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'  # MOVE 'ELEVATED' TO ws_risk_category
        case _:
            ws.ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'  # MOVE 'HIGH RISK' TO ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms) -> None:
    """10400-determine_approval."""
    if ws.ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws.ws_approval_status = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO ws_approval_status
        ws.ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'  # MOVE 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW' TO ws_conditions
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if ws.ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws.ws_approval_status = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO ws_approval_status
        ws.ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'  # MOVE 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED' TO ws_conditions
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if ws.ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws.ws_approval_status = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO ws_approval_status
        ws.ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'  # MOVE 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH' TO ws_conditions
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    ws.ws_approval_status = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO ws_approval_status
    calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws, loan_terms)  # PERFORM 10450-calculate_approved_terms

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms) -> None:
    """10450-calculate_approved_terms."""
    loan_terms.ws_approved_amount = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount  # MOVE ws_loan_amount TO ws_approved_amount
    match ws.ws_credit_tier:
        case 'A':
            loan_terms.ws_approved_rate = ws.ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')  # COMPUTE ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 0.00
        case 'B':
            loan_terms.ws_approved_rate = ws.ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')  # COMPUTE ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 0.50
        case 'C':
            loan_terms.ws_approved_rate = ws.ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')  # COMPUTE ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 1.50
        case 'D':
            loan_terms.ws_approved_rate = ws.ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')  # COMPUTE ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + 3.00
    if ws.ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        loan_terms.ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')  # ADD 0.50 TO ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms) -> None:
    """10500-generate_loan_terms."""
    loan_terms.ws_loan_interest_rate = loan_terms.ws_approved_rate  # MOVE ws_approved_rate TO ws_loan_interest_rate
    loan_terms.ws_monthly_rate = loan_terms.ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200  # COMPUTE ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    loan_terms.ws_compound_factor = (1 + loan_terms.ws_monthly_rate) ** ws.ws_loan_term_months  # COMPUTE ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    loan_terms.ws_loan_monthly_pmt = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount * loan_terms.ws_monthly_rate * loan_terms.ws_compound_factor / (loan_terms.ws_compound_factor - 1)  # COMPUTE ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    loan_terms.ws_loan_principal_bal = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount  # MOVE ws_loan_amount TO ws_loan_principal_bal

def create_amortization_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms, loan_mortgage: LoanMortgage, amortization_schedule: AmortizationSchedule, trade_flags: TradeFlags) -> None:
    """10600-create_amortization."""
    ws.ws_running_balance = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount  # MOVE ws_loan_amount TO ws_running_balance
    ws.ws_payment_date = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_payment_date
    ws.ws_amort_idx = 1
    while ws.ws_amort_idx <= ws.ws_loan_term_months:
        calculate_payment_split_C7(ws, loan_terms, loan_mortgage, amortization_schedule, trade_flags)  # PERFORM 10650-calculate_payment_split
        ws.ws_amort_idx += 1

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms, loan_mortgage: LoanMortgage, amortization_schedule: AmortizationSchedule, trade_flags: TradeFlags) -> None:
    """10650-calculate_payment_split."""
    amortization_schedule.amort_interest[ws.ws_amort_idx] = ws.ws_running_balance * loan_terms.ws_monthly_rate  # COMPUTE amort_interest(ws_amort_idx) = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amortization_schedule.amort_principal[ws.ws_amort_idx] = loan_terms.ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amortization_schedule.amort_interest[ws.ws_amort_idx]  # COMPUTE amort_principal(ws_amort_idx) = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest(ws_amort_idx)
    ws.ws_running_balance -= amortization_schedule.amort_principal[ws.ws_amort_idx]  # SUBTRACT amort_principal(ws_amort_idx) FROM ws_running_balance
    amortization_schedule.amort_balance[ws.ws_amort_idx] = ws.ws_running_balance  # MOVE ws_running_balance TO amort_balance(ws_amort_idx)
    amortization_schedule.amort_payment_num[ws.ws_amort_idx] = ws.ws_amort_idx  # MOVE ws_amort_idx TO amort_payment_num(ws_amort_idx)
    amortization_schedule.amort_payment_amt[ws.ws_amort_idx] = loan_terms.ws_loan_monthly_pmt  # MOVE ws_loan_monthly_pmt TO amort_payment_amt(ws_amort_idx)
    if trade_flags.loan_mortgage:
        amortization_schedule.amort_escrow[ws.ws_amort_idx] = (loan_mortgage.ws_property_tax + loan_mortgage.ws_insurance_premium) / 12  # COMPUTE amort_escrow(ws_amort_idx) = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amortization_schedule.amort_total_pmt[ws.ws_amort_idx] = loan_terms.ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amortization_schedule.amort_escrow[ws.ws_amort_idx] + ws.ws_pmi_amount  # COMPUTE amort_total_pmt(ws_amort_idx) = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow(ws_amort_idx) + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amortization_schedule.amort_total_pmt[ws.ws_amort_idx] = loan_terms.ws_loan_monthly_pmt  # MOVE ws_loan_monthly_pmt TO amort_total_pmt(ws_amort_idx)
    advance_payment_date_C7(ws, amortization_schedule)  # PERFORM 10660-advance_payment_date

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, amortization_schedule: AmortizationSchedule) -> None:
    """10660-advance_payment_date."""
    ws.ws_payment_month += 1  # ADD 1 TO ws_payment_month
    if ws.ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws.ws_payment_month = 1  # MOVE 1 TO ws_payment_month
        ws.ws_payment_year += 1  # ADD 1 TO ws_payment_year
    amortization_schedule.amort_payment_date[ws.ws_amort_idx] = ws.ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws.ws_payment_month * 100 + 1  # COMPUTE amort_payment_date(ws_amort_idx) = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 01

def finalize_loan_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms, loan_record: LoanRecord) -> None:
    """10700-finalize_loan."""
    ws.ws_loan_start_date = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_loan_start_date
    ws.ws_loan_end_date = ws.ws_loan_start_date + (ws.ws_loan_term_months * 30)  # COMPUTE ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + (ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    ws.ws_loan_status = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO ws_loan_status
    create_loan_record_C7(ws, loan_terms, loan_record)  # PERFORM 10750-create_loan_record
    disburse_funds_C7(ws, loan_terms)  # PERFORM 10760-disburse_funds
    send_confirmation_C7(ws)  # PERFORM 10770-send_confirmation

def create_loan_record_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms, loan_record: LoanRecord) -> None:
    """10750-create_loan_record."""
    loan_record.loan_rec_id = ws.ws_loan_id  # MOVE ws_loan_id TO loan_rec_id
    loan_record.loan_rec_type = ws.ws_loan_type  # MOVE ws_loan_type TO loan_rec_type
    loan_record.loan_rec_amount = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount  # MOVE ws_loan_amount TO loan_rec_amount
    loan_record.loan_rec_rate = loan_terms.ws_loan_interest_rate  # MOVE ws_loan_interest_rate TO loan_rec_rate
    loan_record.loan_rec_payment = loan_terms.ws_loan_monthly_pmt  # MOVE ws_loan_monthly_pmt TO loan_rec_payment
    loan_record.loan_rec_start = ws.ws_loan_start_date  # MOVE ws_loan_start_date TO loan_rec_start
    loan_record.loan_rec_status = ws.ws_loan_status  # MOVE ws_loan_status TO loan_rec_status
    #WRITE loan_record FROM ws_loan_record.
    print("Writing loan record:", loan_record)

def disburse_funds_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, loan_terms: LoanTerms) -> None:
    """10760-disburse_funds."""
    ws.ws_disbursement_amount = loan_terms.ws_loan_amount  # MOVE ws_loan_amount TO ws_disbursement_amount
    process_deposit_C7(ws)  # PERFORM 2300-process_deposit
    write_audit_trail_C7(ws)  # PERFORM 2380-write_audit_trail

def send_confirmation_C7(ws: WorkingStorage) -> None:
    """10770-send_confirmation."""
    ws.ws_notif_type = 'loan_confirm'  # MOVE 'loan_confirm' TO ws_notif_type
    ws.ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'  # MOVE 'EMAIL' TO ws_notif_channel
    ws.ws_notif_subject = 'Your loan has been approved'  # MOVE 'Your loan has been approved' TO ws_notif_subject
    send_notification_C7(ws)  # PERFORM 15000-send_notification

def process_decline_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, decline_record: DeclineRecord) -> None:
    """10800-process_decline."""
    ws.ws_loan_status = 'DECLINED'  # MOVE 'DECLINED' TO ws_loan_status
    record_decline_C7(ws, decline_record)  # PERFORM 10810-record_decline
    send_decline_notice_C7(ws)  # PERFORM 10820-send_decline_notice

def record_decline_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, decline_record: DeclineRecord) -> None:
    """10810-record_decline."""
    decline_record.decline_loan_id = ws.ws_loan_id  # MOVE ws_loan_id TO decline_loan_id
    decline_record.decline_status = ws.ws_approval_status  # MOVE ws_approval_status TO decline_status
    decline_record.decline_reason = ws.ws_conditions  # MOVE ws_conditions TO decline_reason
    decline_record.decline_date = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO decline_date
    #WRITE decline_record FROM ws_decline_record.
    print("Writing decline record:", decline_record)

def send_decline_notice_C7(ws: WorkingStorage) -> None:
    """10820-send_decline_notice."""
    ws.ws_notif_type = 'loan_decline'  # MOVE 'loan_decline' TO ws_notif_type
    ws.ws_notif_channel = 'LETTER'  # MOVE 'LETTER' TO ws_notif_channel
    ws.ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your loan application'  # MOVE 'Regarding your loan application' TO ws_notif_subject
    send_notification_C7(ws)  # PERFORM 15000-send_notification

def portfolio_management_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, holdings_file: HoldingsFile, quote_request: QuoteRequest, quote_response: QuoteResponse, report_record: ReportRecord) -> None:
    """11000-portfolio_management."""
    load_portfolio_C7(ws, holdings_file)  # PERFORM 11100-load_portfolio
    update_market_prices_C7(ws, holdings_file, quote_request, quote_response)  # PERFORM 11200-update_market_prices
    calculate_values_C7(ws, holdings_file)  # PERFORM 11300-calculate_values
    rebalance_check_C7(ws, holdings_file)  # PERFORM 11400-rebalance_check
    generate_statements_C7(ws, holdings_file, report_record)  # PERFORM 11500-generate_statements

def load_portfolio_C7(ws: WorkingStorage, holdings_file: HoldingsFile) -> None:
    """11100-load_portfolio."""
    ws.ws_hold_idx = 1  # MOVE 1 TO ws_hold_idx
    ws.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while not (ws.ws_hold_idx > 100 or ws.ws_eof_flag == 'Y'):
        #READ holdings_file INTO ws_holding_rec
        if ws.ws_hold_idx > len(holdings_file.hold_symbol) - 1:
          ws.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
          ws.ws_holding_rec = "Simulated holding rec"
          #move data from holdings_file to ws_holding
          holdings_file.hold_symbol[ws.ws_hold_idx] = "SYM"+str(ws.ws_hold_idx)
          holdings_file.hold_shares[ws.ws_hold_idx] = ws.ws_hold_idx * 10
          holdings_file.hold_cost_per_share[ws.ws_hold_idx] = Decimal(str(ws.ws_hold_idx) + ".99")
          holdings_file.hold_current_price[ws.ws_hold_idx] = Decimal(str(ws.ws_hold_idx) + ".99")
          holdings_file.hold_type[ws.ws_hold_idx] = "STK"
          holdings_file.hold_purchase_date[ws.ws_hold_idx] = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))
          ws.ws_holding_rec_dummy = "ws_holding("+str(ws.ws_hold_idx)+")"
          ws.ws_hold_idx += 1  # ADD 1 TO ws_hold_idx

    ws.ws_holdings_count = ws.ws_hold_idx - 1  # SUBTRACT 1 FROM ws_hold_idx GIVING ws_holdings_count

def _13220_calc_auto_premium_C8() -> None:
    """13220-calc_auto_premium."""
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_DRIVER_AGE, WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR, WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE, WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR, WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM

    match WS_POLICY_TYPE:
        case 'SEDAN':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(500)
        case 'SUV':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(700)
        case 'TRUCK':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(800)
        case _:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(600)

    match WS_DRIVER_AGE:
        case age if 16 <= age <= 25:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal(300)
        case age if 26 <= age <= 40:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal(150)
        case age if 41 <= age <= 65:
            pass
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal(100)
        case _:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal(50)

    if WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)

    if WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
        WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * Decimal(200)
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM += WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE

    if WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
        WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * Decimal(100)
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM += WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE

    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)

def _13230_calc_home_premium_C8() -> None:
    """13230-calc_home_premium."""
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT, WS_HOME_AGE, WS_FLOOD_ZONE, WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM, WS_DEDUCTIBLE, WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM

    WS_BASE_PREMIUM = WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal(0.003)

    match WS_HOME_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.2)
        case _:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)

    if WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)

    if WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)

    WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal(1000) * Decimal(50)
    WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT

    if WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200:
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(200)

    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)

def _13240_calc_health_premium_C8() -> None:
    """13240-calc_health_premium."""
    global WS_BASE_PREMIUM, WS_INSURED_AGE, WS_PLAN_TYPE, WS_FAMILY_PLAN, WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM

    WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(300)

    match WS_INSURED_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.5)
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.3)
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.6)
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.0)
        case _:
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.8)

    match WS_PLAN_TYPE:
        case 'BRONZE':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.8)
        case 'SILVER':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case 'GOLD':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.3)
        case 'PLATINUM':
            WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.6)

    if WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
        WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.5)

    WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal(12)

def _13300_underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """13300-UNDERWRITING."""
    _13310_evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    _13320_check_medical_history_C8()
    _13330_verify_information_C8()
    _13340_determine_decision_C8()

def _13310_evaluate_risk_factors_C8() -> None:
    """13310-evaluate_risk_factors."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_BMI, WS_SMOKER_FLAG, WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION, WS_DRIVER_AGE, WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR

    WS_RISK_POINTS = 0

    if isinstance(POLICY_LIFE, PolicyLife):
        if WS_BMI > 30:
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        if WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
        if WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

    if isinstance(POLICY_AUTO, PolicyAuto):
        if WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        if WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
            WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

def _13320_check_medical_history_C8() -> None:
    """13320-check_medical_history."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_CONDITION_POINTS, WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS, WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION, WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT

    if WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
        WS_CONDITION_POINTS = WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * Decimal(5)
        WS_RISK_POINTS += int(WS_CONDITION_POINTS)

    if WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

    if WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 5

def _13330_verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """13330-verify_information."""
    _13335_check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    _13336_validate_documents_C8()

def _13335_check_fraud_indicators_C8() -> None:
    """13335-check_fraud_indicators."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_RECENT_CLAIMS, WS_FRAUD_FLAG, WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH

    if WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'

    if WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
        WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

def _13336_validate_documents_C8() -> None:
    """13336-validate_documents."""
    global WS_DOC_MISSING, WS_UW_STATUS

    if WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
        WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
    else:
        WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'

def _13340_determine_decision_C8() -> None:
    """13340-determine_decision."""
    global WS_RISK_POINTS, WS_UW_DECISION, WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM

    match True:
        case WS_RISK_POINTS > 50:
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
        case WS_RISK_POINTS > 30:
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
        case WS_RISK_POINTS > 15:
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
        case _:
            WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
            WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)

def _13430_set_beneficiaries_C8() -> None:
    """13430-set_beneficiaries."""
    global WS_BENEF_IDX, BENEF_NAME, BENEF_RELATION, BENEF_PCT, WS_POLICY_NUMBER, WS_BENEFICIARY_REC, BENEF_REC_POLICY, BENEF_REC_NAME, BENEF_REC_RELATION, BENEF_REC_PCT

    WS_BENEF_IDX = 1
    while WS_BENEF_IDX <= 5:
        if BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1].strip() != "":
            WS_BENEFICIARY_REC = ""
            BENEF_REC_POLICY = WS_POLICY_NUMBER
            BENEF_REC_NAME = BENEF_NAME[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            BENEF_REC_RELATION = BENEF_RELATION[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            BENEF_REC_PCT = BENEF_PCT[WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            #WRITE beneficiary_record FROM ws_beneficiary_rec. (Not implemented as no file access)
        WS_BENEF_IDX += 1

    if WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT > WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal(0.8):

def _13540_adjudicate_claim_C8() -> None:
    """13540-adjudicate_claim."""
    global WS_CLAIM_STATUS, WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT, WS_DEDUCTIBLE, WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT, WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT

    if WS_CLAIM_STATUS != 'DENIED':
        WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT - WS_DEDUCTIBLE
        if WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT > WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT:
            WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
        WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'APPROVED'

def check_adverse_media_C9(ws_customer_name: str, media_request: MediaRequest, media_response: MediaResponse, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> int:
    """16116-check_adverse_media.
    """
    media_search_name: str = ws_customer_name
    # CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING media_request media_response
    media_hits_found: int = call_mediasrch(media_request, media_response)  # Assuming call_mediasrch returns the number of hits
    if media_hits_found > 0:
        ws_watchlist_hits += media_hits_found
    return ws_watchlist_hits

def calculate_match_score_C9(ws_ofac_score: int, ws_pep_score: int, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> int:
    """16120-calculate_match_score.
    """
    ws_match_score: int = 0
    if ws_ofac_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score
    if ws_pep_score > 0:
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score
    if ws_watchlist_hits != 0:
      ws_match_score = ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits
    return ws_match_score

def determine_disposition_C9(ws_match_score: int) -> tuple[str, Optional[str]]:
    """16130-determine_disposition.
    """
    ws_match_type: str
    ws_sar_required: str = 'N' # Default value for when not required
    ws_case_status: Optional[str] = None

    if ws_match_score >= 90:
        ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
        ws_sar_required = 'Y'
    elif ws_match_score >= 75:
        ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
        ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
    elif ws_match_score >= 50:
        ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
    else:
        ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
        ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'

    return ws_match_type, ws_sar_required, ws_case_status

def kyc_verification_C9(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """16200-kyc_verification.
    """
    ws_id_status: str
    ws_addr_status: str
    ws_doc_status: str

    ws_id_status = verify_identity_C9(ws_customer_ssn, ws_customer_dob, ws_customer_name)
    ws_addr_status = verify_address_C9(ws_customer_address)
    ws_doc_status = verify_documents_C9(ws_doc_type, ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country, ws_license_number, ws_license_state)
    ws_kyc_status: str = determine_kyc_status_C9(ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status)
    
    return ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status, ws_kyc_status

def verify_identity_C9(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str) -> str:
    """16210-verify_identity.
    """
    id_verify_ssn: str = ws_customer_ssn
    id_verify_dob: str = ws_customer_dob
    id_verify_name: str = ws_customer_name
    # CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING id_request id_response
    id_verified: str = call_idverify(id_verify_ssn, id_verify_dob, id_verify_name) # Assuming call_idverify returns 'Y' or 'N'
    if id_verified == 'Y':
        ws_id_status: str = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        ws_id_status: str = 'FAILED'
    return ws_id_status

def verify_address_C9(ws_customer_address: str) -> str:
    """16220-verify_address.
    """
    addr_verify_input: str = ws_customer_address
    # CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING addr_request addr_response
    addr_verified: str = call_addrverify(addr_verify_input)  # Assuming call_addrverify returns 'Y' or 'N'
    if addr_verified == 'Y':
        ws_addr_status: str = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        ws_addr_status: str = 'UNVERIFIED'
    return ws_addr_status

def verify_documents_C9(ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """16230-verify_documents.
    """
    if ws_doc_type == 'PASSPORT':
        ws_doc_status: str = verify_passport_C9(ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country)
    elif ws_doc_type == 'LICENSE':
        ws_doc_status: str = verify_license_C9(ws_license_number, ws_license_state)
    else:
        ws_doc_status: str = verify_other_doc_C9()
    return ws_doc_status

def verify_passport_C9(ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str) -> str:
    """16232-verify_passport.
    """
    passport_verify_num: str = ws_passport_number
    passport_verify_country: str = ws_passport_country
    # CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING passport_req passport_resp
    passport_valid: str = call_passverify(passport_verify_num, passport_verify_country)  # Assuming call_passverify returns 'Y' or 'N'
    if passport_valid == 'Y':
        ws_doc_status: str = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        ws_doc_status: str = 'INVALID'
    return ws_doc_status

def verify_license_C9(ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str) -> str:
    """16234-verify_license.
    """
    license_verify_num: str = ws_license_number
    license_verify_state: str = ws_license_state
    # CALL 'LICVERIFY' USING license_req license_resp
    license_valid: str = call_licverify(license_verify_num, license_verify_state)  # Assuming call_licverify returns 'Y' or 'N'
    if license_valid == 'Y':
        ws_doc_status: str = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        ws_doc_status: str = 'INVALID'
    return ws_doc_status

def verify_other_doc_C9() -> str:
    """16236-verify_other_doc.
    """
    ws_doc_status: str = 'MANUAL REVIEW'
    return ws_doc_status

def determine_kyc_status_C9(ws_id_status: str, ws_addr_status: str, ws_doc_status: str) -> str:
    """16240-determine_kyc_status.
    """
    if ws_id_status == 'VERIFIED' and ws_addr_status == 'VERIFIED' and ws_doc_status == 'VERIFIED':
        ws_kyc_status: str = 'APPROVED'
    else:
        ws_kyc_status: str = 'PENDING'
    return ws_kyc_status

def sanctions_check_C9(ws_sanctions_hit: str, ws_customer_id: str, account_record: AccountRecord) -> None:
    """16300-sanctions_check.
    """
    if ws_sanctions_hit == 'Y':
        escalate_to_compliance_C9(ws_customer_id)
        freeze_account_C9(account_record)

def escalate_to_compliance_C9(ws_customer_id: str) -> None:
    """16310-escalate_to_compliance.
    """
    ws_escalation_record: EscalationRecord = EscalationRecord()  # Assuming INITIALIZE means creating a new object
    esc_reason: str = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
    esc_customer: str = ws_customer_id
    esc_date: str = get_current_date() # Assuming FUNCTION current_date returns a string
    esc_priority: str = 'URGENT'
    # WRITE escalation_record FROM ws_escalation_record
    write_escalation_record(ws_escalation_record)

def freeze_account_C9(account_record: AccountRecord) -> None:
    """16320-freeze_account.
    """
    ws_account_status: str = 'F'
    ws_freeze_reason: str = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'
    # REWRITE account_record.
    rewrite_account_record(account_record)

def transaction_monitoring_C9(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal, ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str) -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str]:
    """16400-transaction_monitoring.
    """
    ws_velocity_flag: str
    ws_amount_flag: str
    ws_pattern_flag: str
    ws_location_flag: str
    ws_device_flag: str
    
    ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag = check_velocity_C9(ws_daily_trans_count, ws_velocity_threshold, ws_daily_trans_amount, ws_amount_threshold)
    ws_pattern_flag = check_patterns_C9(ws_round_amount_count, ws_structuring_detected)
    ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag = check_high_risk_C9(ws_high_risk_country, ws_new_device)
    ws_fraud_decision: str = calculate_risk_score_C9()
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_pattern_flag, ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_decision

def check_velocity_C9(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16410-check_velocity.
    """
    ws_fraud_score: int = 0
    ws_velocity_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_amount_flag: str = 'N'
    
    if ws_daily_trans_count > ws_velocity_threshold:
        ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    if ws_daily_trans_amount > ws_amount_threshold:
        ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 20
    
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score

def check_patterns_C9(ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str) -> str:
    """16420-check_patterns.
    """
    ws_fraud_score: int = 0
    ws_pattern_flag: str = 'N'
    
    if ws_round_amount_count > 5:
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 15
    if ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 30
    
    return ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score

def check_high_risk_C9(ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16430-check_high_risk.
    """
    ws_fraud_score: int = 0
    ws_location_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_device_flag: str = 'N'
    
    if ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':
        ws_location_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 25
    if ws_new_device == 'Y':
        ws_device_flag = 'Y'
        ws_fraud_score += 10
    
    return ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score

def calculate_risk_score_C9() -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16440-calculate_risk_score.
    """
    ws_fraud_score: int
    ws_fraud_decision: str
    ws_manual_review: str = 'N'
    
    if ws_fraud_score >= 80:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'
        ws_manual_review = 'Y'
    elif ws_fraud_score >= 60:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'
        ws_manual_review = 'Y'
    elif ws_fraud_score >= 40:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'
    else:
        ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'
        
    return ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

def suspicious_activity_report_C9(ws_sar_required: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """16500-suspicious_activity_report.

    if ws_sar_required == 'Y':
        gather_sar_data_C9(ws_customer_name, ws_customer_address, ws_customer_ssn, ws_transaction_amount)
        generate_sar_C9()
        file_sar_C9()

def customer_service_C9() -> None:
    """17000-customer_service.
    """
    create_case_C9()
    route_case_C9()
    process_case_C9()
    resolve_case_C9()
    follow_up_C9()

def create_case_C9(ws_case_type: str) -> None:
    """17100-create_case.
    """
    generate_case_id_C9()
    ws_open_date: str = get_current_date() # Assuming FUNCTION current_date returns a string
    ws_case_status: str = 'OPEN'
    categorize_case_C9(ws_case_type, ws_open_date)

def generate_case_id_C9() -> None:
    """17110-generate_case_id.
    """
    ws_date_part: str = get_current_date() # Assuming FUNCTION current_date returns a string
    ws_random_part: int = int(get_random_number() * 99999) # Assuming FUNCTION RANDOM returns a float between 0 and 1
    #STRING 'CS' DELIMITED SIZE
    #       ws_date_part DELIMITED SIZE
    #       ws_random_part DELIMITED SIZE
    #   INTO ws_case_id.
    ws_case_id: str = f"CS{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"

def categorize_case_C9(ws_case_type: str, ws_open_date: str) -> None:
    """17120-categorize_case.
    """
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority: int = 2
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_case_priority = 1
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_case_priority = 1
        case 'GENERAL INQUIRY':
            ws_case_priority = 3
        case _:
            ws_case_priority = 3
    ws_target_date: int = calculate_integer_of_date(ws_open_date) + ws_case_priority * 2

def route_case_C9(ws_case_type: str) -> None:
    """17200-route_case.
    """
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            ws_queue: str = 'BILLING'
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            ws_queue = 'FRAUD'
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            ws_queue = 'SECURITY'
        case 'LOAN INQUIRY':
            ws_queue = 'LENDING'
        case _:
            ws_queue = 'GENERAL'
    assign_agent_C9(ws_queue)

def assign_agent_C9(ws_queue: str) -> None:
    """17210-assign_agent.
    """
    #CALL 'ROUTECASE' USING ws_queue ws_assigned_agent
    ws_assigned_agent: str = call_routecase(ws_queue)  # Assuming call_routecase returns the assigned agent or spaces
    if ws_assigned_agent == '':  # Assuming SPACES is an empty string
        ws_case_status: str = 'UNASSIGNED'
    else:
        ws_case_status: str = 'ASSIGNED'

def process_case_C9(ws_channel: str, ws_assigned_agent: str, ws_customer_account: str, ws_customer_id: str) -> None:
    """17300-process_case.
    """
    log_interaction_C9(ws_channel, ws_assigned_agent)
    research_issue_C9(ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id)
    determine_resolution_C9()

def log_interaction_C9(ws_channel: str, ws_assigned_agent: str) -> None:
    """17310-log_interaction.
    """
    ws_interaction_count: int = 0
    ws_interaction_count += 1
    int_date: list[str] = [] # Assuming int_date is an array
    int_time: list[str] = [] # Assuming int_time is an array
    int_channel: list[str] = [] # Assuming int_channel is an array
    int_agent: list[str] = [] # Assuming int_agent is an array
    
    int_date.append(get_current_date()) # Assuming FUNCTION current_date returns a string
    int_time.append(get_current_time()) # Assuming FUNCTION current_time returns a string
    int_channel.append(ws_channel)
    int_agent.append(ws_assigned_agent)

def research_issue_C9(ws_customer_account: str, ws_customer_id: str) -> None:
    """17320-research_issue.
    """
    pull_account_history_C9(ws_customer_account)
    check_previous_cases_C9(ws_customer_id)
    review_notes_C9()

def pull_account_history_C9(ws_customer_account: str) -> None:
    """17322-pull_account_history.
    """
    hist_search_key: str = ws_customer_account
    #READ history_file INTO ws_account_history
    #   KEY IS hist_account
    #   INVALID KEY
    #      MOVE 'NO HISTORY FOUND' TO ws_research_notes
    #end_read.
    ws_account_history: HistoryFile = read_history_file(hist_search_key)
    if ws_account_history is None:
        ws_research_notes: str = 'NO HISTORY FOUND'

def check_previous_cases_C9(ws_customer_id: str) -> None:
    """17324-check_previous_cases.
    """
    case_search_key: str = ws_customer_id
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_previous_case_count: int = 0
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        #READ case_file INTO ws_previous_case
        #   KEY IS case_customer
        #   AT END
        #      MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        #   NOT AT END
        #      ADD 1 TO ws_previous_case_count
        #end_read
        ws_previous_case: CaseFile = read_case_file(case_search_key)
        if ws_previous_case is None:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_previous_case_count += 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def review_notes_C9() -> None:
    """17326-review_notes.
    """
    ws_previous_case_count: int
    if ws_previous_case_count > 0:
        ws_caller_type: str = 'REPEAT CALLER'
    else:
        ws_caller_type: str = 'FIRST CONTACT'

def determine_resolution_C9(ws_case_type: str, ws_billing_error: str, ws_customer_account: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """17330-determine_resolution.
    """
    match ws_case_type:
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY':
            resolve_billing_C9(ws_billing_error, ws_customer_account, ws_credit_amount)
        case 'FRAUD REPORT':
            resolve_fraud_C9(ws_customer_account)
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS':
            resolve_access_C9(ws_customer_account)
        case _:
            resolve_general_C9()

def resolve_billing_C9(ws_billing_error: str, ws_customer_account: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """17332-resolve_billing.
    """
    if ws_billing_error == 'Y':
        issue_credit_C9(ws_customer_account, ws_credit_amount)
        ws_resolution_code: str = 'CREDIT ISSUED'
    else:
        ws_resolution_code: str = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

    def read(self):

            return None

DASH_LIQUIDITY: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ACCT_LAST_ACTIVITY: str = ""
ACCT_STATUS: str = ""
