def data_analytics_C10():
    """COBOL: 21000-data_analytics."""
    collect_metrics_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21100-collect_metrics
    aggregate_data_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21200-aggregate_data
    calculate_kpi_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21300-calculate_kpi
    generate_dashboard_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21400-generate_dashboard
    export_data_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21500-export_data

def collect_metrics_C10():
    """COBOL: 21100-collect_metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21110-collect_transaction_metrics
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21120-collect_customer_metrics
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21130-collect_performance_metrics

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10():
    """COBOL: 21110-collect_transaction_metrics."""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, TRANS_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_amount
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_count
    WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_avg_trans_amount
    transaction_file = TransactionFile()  # Initialize the dummy file
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        ws_trans_rec = transaction_file.read() # COBOL: READ transaction_file INTO ws_trans_rec
        if ws_trans_rec is None: # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else: # COBOL: NOT AT END
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_total_trans_count
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += TRANS_AMOUNT  # COBOL: ADD trans_amount TO ws_total_trans_amount
    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_total_trans_count > 0
        WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def collect_customer_metrics_C10():
    """COBOL: 21120-collect_customer_metrics."""
    global WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS, WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS, WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS, WS_EOF_FLAG, CUST_STATUS, CUST_OPEN_DATE, CUST_CLOSE_DATE, WS_PERIOD_START
    WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_active_customers
    WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_new_customers
    WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_churned_customers
    customer_file = CustomerFile() # Initialize dummy file
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        ws_cust_rec = customer_file.read()  # COBOL: READ customer_file INTO ws_cust_rec
        if ws_cust_rec is None:  # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else:  # COBOL: NOT AT END
            if CUST_STATUS == 'A':  # COBOL: IF cust_status = 'A'
                WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS += 1  # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_active_customers
            if CUST_OPEN_DATE >= WS_PERIOD_START:  # COBOL: IF cust_open_date >= ws_period_start
                WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS += 1  # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_new_customers
            if CUST_CLOSE_DATE >= WS_PERIOD_START:  # COBOL: IF cust_close_date >= ws_period_start
                WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS += 1  # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_churned_customers
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def collect_performance_metrics_C10():
    """COBOL: 21130-collect_performance_metrics."""
    global WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL, WS_RESPONSE_COUNT, WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME, WS_EOF_FLAG, PERF_RESPONSE_TIME
    WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_response_time_total
    WS_RESPONSE_COUNT = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_response_count
    perf_log_file = PerfLogFile() # Initialize dummy file
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        ws_perf_rec = perf_log_file.read()  # COBOL: READ perf_log_file INTO ws_perf_rec
        if ws_perf_rec is None:  # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else:  # COBOL: NOT AT END
            WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL += PERF_RESPONSE_TIME  # COBOL: ADD perf_response_time TO ws_response_time_total
            WS_RESPONSE_COUNT += 1  # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_response_count
    if WS_RESPONSE_COUNT > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_response_count > 0
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME = WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL / WS_RESPONSE_COUNT  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def aggregate_data_C10():
    """COBOL: 21200-aggregate_data."""
    daily_aggregation_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21210-daily_aggregation
    weekly_aggregation_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21220-weekly_aggregation
    monthly_aggregation_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21230-monthly_aggregation

def daily_aggregation_C10():
    """COBOL: 21210-daily_aggregation."""
    global WS_DAILY_SUMMARY, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS, DAILY_DATE, DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT, DAILY_DEPOSITS, DAILY_WITHDRAWALS

    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY = WsDailySummary() # COBOL: INITIALIZE ws_daily_summary
    DAILY_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE  # COBOL: MOVE ws_process_date TO daily_date
    DAILY_TRANS_COUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # COBOL: MOVE ws_total_trans_count TO daily_trans_count
    DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT  # COBOL: MOVE ws_total_trans_amount TO daily_trans_amount
    DAILY_DEPOSITS = WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS  # COBOL: MOVE ws_total_deposits TO daily_deposits
    DAILY_WITHDRAWALS = WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS  # COBOL: MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO daily_withdrawals
    # Assuming a function to write the record exists
    write_daily_summary_record(WS_DAILY_SUMMARY)  # COBOL: WRITE daily_summary_record FROM ws_daily_summary

def weekly_aggregation_C10():
    """COBOL: 21220-weekly_aggregation."""
    global WS_DAY_OF_WEEK, WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY, WS_WEEK_NUMBER, WEEKLY_WEEK

    if WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7:  # COBOL: IF ws_day_of_week = 7
        WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY = WsWeeklySummary() # COBOL: INITIALIZE ws_weekly_summary
        WEEKLY_WEEK = WS_WEEK_NUMBER  # COBOL: MOVE ws_week_number TO weekly_week
        sum_week_data_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21225-sum_week_data
        write_weekly_summary_record(WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY)  # COBOL: WRITE weekly_summary_record FROM ws_weekly_summary

def sum_week_data_C10():
    """COBOL: 21225-sum_week_data."""
    global WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT, WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT, DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT

    WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_count
    WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_amount
    for _ in range(7):  # COBOL: PERFORM 7 TIMES
        WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT += DAILY_TRANS_COUNT  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_count TO weekly_trans_count
        WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_amount TO weekly_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10():
    """COBOL: 21230-monthly_aggregation."""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH, WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY, WS_CURR_MONTH, WS_CURR_YEAR, MONTHLY_MONTH, MONTHLY_YEAR

    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':  # COBOL: IF ws_end_of_month = 'Y'
        WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY = WsMonthlySummary()  # COBOL: INITIALIZE ws_monthly_summary
        MONTHLY_MONTH = WS_CURR_MONTH  # COBOL: MOVE ws_curr_month TO monthly_month
        MONTHLY_YEAR = WS_CURR_YEAR  # COBOL: MOVE ws_curr_year TO monthly_year
        sum_month_data_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21235-sum_month_data
        write_monthly_summary_record(WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY)  # COBOL: WRITE monthly_summary_record FROM ws_monthly_summary

def sum_month_data_C10():
    """COBOL: 21235-sum_month_data."""
    global MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT, MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT, MONTHLY_NEW_ACCOUNTS, MONTHLY_CLOSED_ACCOUNTS, WS_EOF_FLAG, DAILY_MONTH, WS_CURR_MONTH, DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT

    MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_count
    MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_amount
    MONTHLY_NEW_ACCOUNTS = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_new_accounts
    MONTHLY_CLOSED_ACCOUNTS = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_closed_accounts
    daily_summary_file = DailySummaryFile()  # Initialize the dummy file
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.read()  # COBOL: READ daily_summary_file INTO ws_daily_sum_rec
        if ws_daily_sum_rec is None:  # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else:  # COBOL: NOT AT END
            if DAILY_MONTH == WS_CURR_MONTH:  # COBOL: IF daily_month = ws_curr_month
                MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT += DAILY_TRANS_COUNT  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_count TO monthly_trans_count
                MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_amount TO monthly_trans_amount
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def calculate_kpi_C10():
    """COBOL: 21300-calculate_kpi."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21310-calc_financial_kpi
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21320-calc_operational_kpi
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21330-calc_customer_kpi

def calc_financial_kpi_C10():
    """COBOL: 21310-calc_financial_kpi."""
    global WS_TOTAL_ASSETS, WS_ROA, WS_NET_INCOME, WS_TOTAL_EQUITY, WS_ROE, WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE, WS_NIM, WS_INTEREST_INCOME, WS_EARNING_ASSETS

    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_total_assets > 0
        WS_ROA = (WS_NET_INCOME / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100
    if WS_TOTAL_EQUITY > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_total_equity > 0
        WS_ROE = (WS_NET_INCOME / WS_TOTAL_EQUITY) * 100  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100
    if WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_interest_expense > 0
        WS_NIM = ((WS_INTEREST_INCOME - WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE) / WS_EARNING_ASSETS) * 100  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10():
    """COBOL: 21320-calc_operational_kpi."""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ERROR_RATE, WS_ERROR_COUNT, WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE, WS_WITHIN_SLA_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_CASES, WS_FIRST_CALL_RESOLUTION, WS_FCR_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_CALLS

    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_total_trans_count > 0
        WS_ERROR_RATE = (WS_ERROR_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT) * 100  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE = (WS_WITHIN_SLA_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_CASES) * 100  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    WS_FIRST_CALL_RESOLUTION = (WS_FCR_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_CALLS) * 100  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10():
    """COBOL: 21330-calc_customer_kpi."""
    global WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS, WS_CHURN_RATE, WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS, WS_ACQUISITION_COST, WS_MARKETING_SPEND, WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS, WS_LIFETIME_VALUE, WS_AVG_REVENUE_PER_CUSTOMER, WS_AVG_CUSTOMER_TENURE

    if WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_active_customers > 0
        WS_CHURN_RATE = (WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS / WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS) * 100  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100
    WS_ACQUISITION_COST = WS_MARKETING_SPEND / WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    WS_LIFETIME_VALUE = WS_AVG_REVENUE_PER_CUSTOMER * WS_AVG_CUSTOMER_TENURE  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure

def generate_dashboard_C10():
    """COBOL: 21400-generate_dashboard."""
    create_executive_dashboard_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21410-create_executive_dashboard
    create_operations_dashboard_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21420-create_operations_dashboard
    create_risk_dashboard_C10()  # COBOL: PERFORM 21430-create_risk_dashboard

def create_executive_dashboard_C10():
    """COBOL: 21410-create_executive_dashboard."""
    global DASH_TITLE, WS_TOTAL_REVENUE, DASH_REVENUE, WS_NET_INCOME, DASH_NET_INCOME, WS_ROA, DASH_ROA, WS_ROE, DASH_ROE, WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS, DASH_CUSTOMERS, WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD

    DASH_TITLE = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'  # COBOL: MOVE 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD' TO dash_title
    DASH_REVENUE = WS_TOTAL_REVENUE  # COBOL: MOVE ws_total_revenue TO dash_revenue
    DASH_NET_INCOME = WS_NET_INCOME  # COBOL: MOVE ws_net_income TO dash_net_income
    DASH_ROA = WS_ROA  # COBOL: MOVE ws_roa TO dash_roa
    DASH_ROE = WS_ROE  # COBOL: MOVE ws_roe TO dash_roe
    DASH_CUSTOMERS = WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS  # COBOL: MOVE ws_active_customers TO dash_customers
    WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD = WsExecDashboard(DASH_TITLE, DASH_REVENUE, DASH_NET_INCOME, DASH_ROA, DASH_ROE, DASH_CUSTOMERS)
    write_dashboard_record(WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD)  # COBOL: WRITE dashboard_record FROM ws_exec_dashboard

def create_operations_dashboard_C10():
    """COBOL: 21420-create_operations_dashboard."""
    global DASH_TITLE, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, DASH_TRANS_COUNT, WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME, DASH_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_ERROR_RATE, DASH_ERROR_RATE, WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE, DASH_SLA_PCT, WS_OPS_DASHBOARD

    DASH_TITLE = 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD'  # COBOL: MOVE 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD' TO dash_title
    DASH_TRANS_COUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # COBOL: MOVE ws_total_trans_count TO dash_trans_count
    DASH_AVG_RESPONSE = WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME  # COBOL: MOVE ws_avg_response_time TO dash_avg_response
    DASH_ERROR_RATE = WS_ERROR_RATE  # COBOL: MOVE ws_error_rate TO dash_error_rate
    DASH_SLA_PCT = WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE  # COBOL: MOVE ws_sla_compliance TO dash_sla_pct
    WS_OPS_DASHBOARD = WsOpsDashboard(DASH_TITLE, DASH_TRANS_COUNT, DASH_AVG_RESPONSE, DASH_ERROR_RATE, DASH_SLA_PCT)
    write_dashboard_record(WS_OPS_DASHBOARD)  # COBOL: WRITE dashboard_record FROM ws_ops_dashboard

def create_risk_dashboard_C10():
    """COBOL: 21430-create_risk_dashboard."""

    DASH_TITLE = 'RISK DASHBOARD'  # COBOL: MOVE 'RISK DASHBOARD' TO dash_title

    DASH_NPL = WS_NPL_RATIO  # COBOL: MOVE ws_npl_ratio TO dash_npl
    DASH_CAPITAL = WS_CAPITAL_RATIO  # COBOL: MOVE ws_capital_ratio TO dash_capital
    DASH_LIQUIDITY = WS_LIQUIDITY_RATIO  # COBOL: MOVE ws_liquidity_ratio TO dash_liquidity
    WS_RISK_DASHBOARD = WsRiskDashboard(DASH_TITLE, DASH_FRAUD_SCORE, DASH_NPL, DASH_CAPITAL, DASH_LIQUIDITY)

def export_csv_C10():
    """COBOL: 21510-export_csv."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, DAILY_DATE, DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT, DAILY_DEPOSITS, DAILY_WITHDRAWALS, WS_CSV_LINE, WS_CSV_HEADER

    csv_export_file = CSVExportFile()  # COBOL: OPEN OUTPUT csv_export_file
    WS_CSV_HEADER = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals' TO ws_csv_header
    csv_export_file.write(WS_CSV_HEADER)  # COBOL: WRITE csv_record FROM ws_csv_header
    daily_summary_file = DailySummaryFile() # Initialize dummy file
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.read()  # COBOL: READ daily_summary_file INTO ws_daily_sum_rec
        if ws_daily_sum_rec is None:  # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else:  # COBOL: NOT AT END
            WS_CSV_LINE = f"{DAILY_DATE},{DAILY_TRANS_COUNT},{DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT},{DAILY_"

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 23500-card_blocking"""
    global CARD_STATUS, WS_BLOCK_REASON, CARD_BLOCK_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, CARD_BLOCK_DATE, WS_CARD_RECORD, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_BODY
    CARD_STATUS = 'B' # COBOL: MOVE 'B' TO card_status
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON = WS_BLOCK_REASON # COBOL: MOVE ws_block_reason TO card_block_reason
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE # COBOL: MOVE ws_process_date TO card_block_date
    # REWRITE card_record FROM ws_card_record
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked' # COBOL: MOVE 'card_blocked' TO ws_notif_type
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS' # COBOL: MOVE 'SMS' TO ws_notif_channel
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = 'Your card has been blocked: ' + WS_BLOCK_REASON # COBOL: STRING 'Your card has been blocked: ' DELIMITED SIZE ws_block_reason DELIMITED SIZE INTO ws_notif_body
    send_notification_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 15000-send_notification

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24000-wire_transfer"""
    validate_wire_request_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24100-validate_wire_request
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y': # COBOL: IF ws_wire_valid = 'Y'
        ofac_screening_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24200-ofac_screening
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y': # COBOL: IF ws_ofac_clear = 'Y'
            process_wire_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24300-process_wire
            send_confirmation_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24400-send_confirmation
        else: # COBOL: ELSE
            reject_wire_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24500-reject_wire

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24100-validate_wire_request"""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_wire_valid
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0: # COBOL: IF ws_wire_amount <= 0
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_wire_valid
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT' # COBOL: MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO ws_wire_reject
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE: # COBOL: IF ws_wire_amount > ws_account_balance
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_wire_valid
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' # COBOL: MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO ws_wire_reject
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == " " * len(WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT): # COBOL: IF ws_beneficiary_account = SPACES
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_wire_valid
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' # COBOL: MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO ws_wire_reject
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000: # COBOL: IF ws_wire_amount > 10000
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_ctr_required

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24200-ofac_screening"""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE, OFAC_MATCH_FOUND, OFAC_MATCH_SCORE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_ofac_clear
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME = WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # COBOL: MOVE ws_beneficiary_name TO ofac_search_name
    ofacsrch(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE) # COBOL: CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y': # COBOL: IF ofac_match_found = 'Y'
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85: # COBOL: IF ofac_match_score >= 85
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_ofac_clear
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH' # COBOL: MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO ws_wire_reject
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK # COBOL: MOVE ws_beneficiary_bank TO ofac_search_bank
    ofacsrch(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE) # COBOL: CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y': # COBOL: IF ofac_match_found = 'Y'
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85: # COBOL: IF ofac_match_score >= 85
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_ofac_clear
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH' # COBOL: MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO ws_wire_reject

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24300-process_wire"""
    debit_originator_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24310-debit_originator
    create_wire_message_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24320-create_wire_message
    transmit_wire_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24330-transmit_wire
    record_wire_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24340-record_wire

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24310-debit_originator"""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL: SUBTRACT ws_wire_amount FROM ws_account_balance
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_WIRE_FEE # COBOL: SUBTRACT ws_wire_fee FROM ws_account_balance
    update_account_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 2350-update_account

def create_wire_message_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24320-create_wire_message"""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, SWIFT_MSG_TYPE, WS_WIRE_REF, SWIFT_TXN_REF, WS_WIRE_DATE, SWIFT_VALUE_DATE, WS_WIRE_CURRENCY, SWIFT_CURRENCY, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, SWIFT_AMOUNT, WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME, SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST, WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT, SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, SWIFT_BENEF_CUST, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC, SWIFT_BENEF_BANK, WS_PURPOSE, SWIFT_REMIT_INFO
    WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE = WsSwiftMessage() # COBOL: INITIALIZE ws_swift_message
    SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103' # COBOL: MOVE 'MT103' TO swift_msg_type
    SWIFT_TXN_REF = WS_WIRE_REF # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_ref TO swift_txn_ref
    SWIFT_VALUE_DATE = WS_WIRE_DATE # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_date TO swift_value_date
    SWIFT_CURRENCY = WS_WIRE_CURRENCY # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_currency TO swift_currency
    SWIFT_AMOUNT = WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_amount TO swift_amount
    SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST = WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME # COBOL: MOVE ws_originator_name TO swift_ordering_cust
    SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # COBOL: MOVE ws_originator_account TO swift_ordering_acct
    SWIFT_BENEF_CUST = WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # COBOL: MOVE ws_beneficiary_name TO swift_benef_cust
    SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # COBOL: MOVE ws_beneficiary_account TO swift_benef_acct
    SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC # COBOL: MOVE ws_beneficiary_bank_bic TO swift_benef_bank
    SWIFT_REMIT_INFO = WS_PURPOSE # COBOL: MOVE ws_purpose TO swift_remit_info

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24330-transmit_wire"""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE, SWIFT_STATUS, WS_WIRE_STATUS
    swift_send(WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE) # COBOL: CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING ws_swift_message ws_swift_response
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK': # COBOL: IF swift_status = 'ACK'
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT' # COBOL: MOVE 'SENT' TO ws_wire_status
    else: # COBOL: ELSE
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED' # COBOL: MOVE 'FAILED' TO ws_wire_status
        reverse_debit_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 24350-reverse_debit

def record_wire_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24340-record_wire"""
    global WS_WIRE_RECORD, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_WIRE_STATUS, WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WIRE_RECORD
    WS_WIRE_RECORD = WsWireRecord() # COBOL: INITIALIZE ws_wire_record
    WIRE_REF = WS_WIRE_REF # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_ref TO wire_ref
    WIRE_AMOUNT = WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_amount TO wire_amount
    WIRE_STATUS = WS_WIRE_STATUS # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_status TO wire_status
    WIRE_FROM_ACCT = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # COBOL: MOVE ws_originator_account TO wire_from_acct
    WIRE_TO_ACCT = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # COBOL: MOVE ws_beneficiary_account TO wire_to_acct
    WIRE_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE # COBOL: MOVE ws_process_date TO wire_date
    # WRITE wire_record FROM ws_wire_record

def reverse_debit_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24350-reverse_debit"""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # COBOL: ADD ws_wire_amount TO ws_account_balance
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_WIRE_FEE # COBOL: ADD ws_wire_fee TO ws_account_balance
    update_account_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 2350-update_account

def send_confirmation_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24400-send_confirmation"""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_confirm' # COBOL: MOVE 'wire_confirm' TO ws_notif_type
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL' # COBOL: MOVE 'EMAIL' TO ws_notif_channel
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Wire transfer ' + WS_WIRE_REF + ' completed' # COBOL: STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE ws_wire_ref DELIMITED SIZE ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE INTO ws_notif_subject
    send_notification_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 15000-send_notification

def reject_wire_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 24500-reject_wire"""
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS, WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WIRE_REJECT_RECORD, WS_NOTIF_TYPE
    WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED' # COBOL: MOVE 'REJECTED' TO ws_wire_status
    WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC = WsWireRejectRec() # COBOL: INITIALIZE ws_wire_reject_rec
    REJECT_WIRE_REF = WS_WIRE_REF # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_ref TO reject_wire_ref
    REJECT_REASON = WS_WIRE_REJECT # COBOL: MOVE ws_wire_reject TO reject_reason
    REJECT_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE # COBOL: MOVE ws_process_date TO reject_date
    # WRITE wire_reject_record FROM ws_wire_reject_rec
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_rejected' # COBOL: MOVE 'wire_rejected' TO ws_notif_type
    send_notification_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 15000-send_notification

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 25000-ach_processing"""
    receive_ach_file_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 25100-receive_ach_file
    validate_ach_entries_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 25200-validate_ach_entries
    process_ach_credits_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 25300-process_ach_credits
    process_ach_debits_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 25400-process_ach_debits
    generate_ach_return_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 25500-generate_ach_return

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 25100-receive_ach_file"""
    global ACH_INPUT_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER, ACH_FILE_ID, WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE, ACH_CREATION_DATE, WS_ACH_FILE_DATE, ACH_ENTRY_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES
    # OPEN INPUT ach_input_file
    # READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_file_header
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE = ACH_FILE_ID # COBOL: MOVE ach_file_id TO ws_current_ach_file
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE = ACH_CREATION_DATE # COBOL: MOVE ach_creation_date TO ws_ach_file_date
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES = ACH_ENTRY_COUNT # COBOL: MOVE ach_entry_count TO ws_expected_entries

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 25200-validate_ach_entries"""
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_INPUT_FILE, WS_ACH_ENTRY
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_valid_entries
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_invalid_entries
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
        try:
            # Simulate reading a line from ACH_INPUT_FILE into WS_ACH_ENTRY
            # WS_ACH_ENTRY = ACH_INPUT_FILE.readline()
            validate_single_entry_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 25210-validate_single_entry
        except Exception: # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        #COBOL: end_read
    #COBOL: end_perform
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 25210-validate_single_entry"""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, ACH_ROUTING, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, ACH_ACCOUNT, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_ach_entry_valid
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric(): # COBOL: IF ach_routing NOT NUMERIC
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_ach_entry_valid
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03' # COBOL: MOVE 'R03' TO ws_ach_return_code
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == " " * len(ACH_ACCOUNT): # COBOL: IF ach_account = SPACES
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_ach_entry_valid
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04' # COBOL: MOVE 'R04' TO ws_ach_return_code
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0: # COBOL: IF ach_amount <= 0
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_ach_entry_valid
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06' # COBOL: MOVE 'R06' TO ws_ach_return_code
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y': # COBOL: IF ws_ach_entry_valid = 'Y'
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_valid_entries
    else: # COBOL: ELSE
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_invalid_entries

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 25300-process_ach_credits"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_INPUT_FILE, WS_ACH_ENTRY, ACH_TRANS_CODE
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
        try:
            #Simulate reading file
            #WS_ACH_ENTRY = ACH_INPUT_FILE.readline()
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('22', '23', '32', '33'): # COBOL: IF ach_trans_code = '22' OR '23' OR '32' OR '33'
                apply_credit_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 25310-apply_credit
        except Exception: # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        #COBOL: end_read
    #COBOL: end_perform
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def apply_credit_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL: 25310-apply_credit"""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY = ACH_ACCOUNT # COBOL: MOVE ach_account TO ws_search_key
    search_account_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 5000-search_account
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y': # COBOL: IF ws_found_flag = 'Y'
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += ACH_AMOUNT # COBOL: ADD ach_amount TO ws_account_balance
        update_account_C11() # COBOL: PERFORM 2350-update_account
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_credits_posted
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += ACH_AMOUNT # COBOL: ADD ach_amount TO WS

@dataclass
class StopRecord:
    stop_account: str = ""
    stop_check_number: int = 0
    stop_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    stop_payee: str = ""
    stop_effective_date: int = 0
    stop_expiry_date: int = 0
    stop_status: str = ""

@dataclass
class RentalAgreement:
    rental_box_number: int = 0
    rental_customer: str = ""
    rental_start_date: int = 0
    rental_annual_fee: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class AccessLog:
    access_box_number: int = 0
    access_customer: str = ""
    access_date: int = 0
    access_time: str = ""
    access_type: str = ""

@dataclass
class DrillingRecord:
    drill_box_number: int = 0
    drill_reason: str = ""
    drill_scheduled_date: int = 0

@dataclass
class AuthRecord:
    auth_rec_card: str = ""
    auth_rec_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    auth_rec_code: int = 0
    auth_rec_date: int = 0
    auth_rec_time: str = ""
    auth_rec_merchant: str = ""
    auth_rec_status: str = ""

@dataclass
class DeclineRecord:
    decline_rec_card: str = ""
    decline_rec_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    decline_rec_code: str = ""
    decline_rec_date: int = 0

@dataclass
class CaptureRecord:
    capture_card: str = ""
    capture_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    capture_auth_code: int = 0
    capture_date: int = 0
    capture_settled: str = 'N'

@dataclass
class FundingRecord:
    funding_merchant: str = ""
    funding_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    funding_fees: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    funding_date: int = 0

@dataclass
class SettleHeader:
    settle_record_type: str = ""
    settle_merchant_id: str = ""
    settle_date: int = 0

@dataclass
class SettleDetail:
    settle_record_type: str = ""
    settle_card: str = ""
    settle_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    settle_auth_code: int = 0

@dataclass
class SettleTrailer:
    settle_record_type: str = ""
    settle_total_count: int = 0
    settle_total_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class ChargebackRecord:
    cb_card: str = ""
    cb_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cb_reason: str = ""
    cb_case_id: str = ""
    cb_received_date: int = 0
    cb_status: str = ""
    cb_action: str = ""

@dataclass
class FileErrorLog:
    file_err_name: str = ""
    file_err_status: str = ""

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """29100-validate_stop_request."""
    global WS_STOP_VALID, WS_STOP_REJECT, WS_CHECK_NUMBER, WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED

    WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y' #29101
    if WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0: #29102
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N' #29103
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED' #29104
    if WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y': #29105
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N' #29106
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED' #29107

def create_stop_order_C12() -> None:
    """29200-create_stop_order."""
    global WS_STOP_RECORD, ACCT_ID, WS_CHECK_NUMBER, WS_CHECK_AMOUNT, WS_PAYEE_NAME, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    global STOP_RECORD
    WS_STOP_RECORD = StopRecord()
    STOP_ACCOUNT = ACCT_ID #29202
    STOP_CHECK_NUMBER = WS_CHECK_NUMBER #29203
    STOP_AMOUNT = WS_CHECK_AMOUNT #29204
    STOP_PAYEE = WS_PAYEE_NAME #29205
    STOP_EFFECTIVE_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE #29206
    STOP_EXPIRY_DATE = int(WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180 #29207
    STOP_STATUS = 'A' #29208
    write_stop_record(WS_STOP_RECORD) #29209

def apply_stop_fee_C12() -> None:
    """29300-apply_stop_fee."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CHECK_NUMBER

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE #29301
    update_account_C12() #29302
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'stop_payment' #29303
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL' #29304
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check # {WS_CHECK_NUMBER}' #29305
    send_notification_C12() #29306

def safe_deposit_box_C12() -> None:
    """30000-safe_deposit_box."""
    box_rental_C12() #30001
    box_access_C12() #30002
    box_drilling_C12() #30003
    box_billing_C12() #30004

def box_rental_C12() -> None:
    """30100-box_rental."""
    global WS_RENTAL_REQUEST, WS_BOX_AVAILABLE
    if WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y': #30101
        check_availability_C12() #30102
        if WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y': #30103
            assign_box_C12() #30104
            create_rental_agreement_C12() #30105

def check_availability_C12() -> None:
    """30110-check_availability."""
    global WS_BOX_AVAILABLE, WS_BOX_IDX, WS_TOTAL_BOXES, BOX_STATUS, BOX_SIZE, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX
    WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N' #30110
    WS_BOX_IDX = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES: #30111
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX -1] == 'A': #30112
            if BOX_SIZE[WS_BOX_IDX -1] == WS_REQUESTED_SIZE: #30113
                WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y' #30114
                WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = WS_BOX_IDX #30115
                break
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1 #30111

def assign_box_C12() -> None:
    """30120-assign_box."""
    global WS_ASSIGNED_BOX, BOX_STATUS, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, BOX_RENTER, WS_PROCESS_DATE, BOX_RENTAL_DATE
    BOX_STATUS[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = 'R' #30120
    BOX_RENTER[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = WS_CUSTOMER_ID #30121
    BOX_RENTAL_DATE[WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1] = WS_PROCESS_DATE #30122

def create_rental_agreement_C12() -> None:
    """30130-create_rental_agreement."""
    global WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, RENTAL_RECORD
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = RentalAgreement()
    RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER = WS_ASSIGNED_BOX #30131
    RENTAL_CUSTOMER = WS_CUSTOMER_ID #30132
    RENTAL_START_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE #30133
    RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE = WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[WS_REQUESTED_SIZE -1] #30134
    write_rental_record(WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT) #30135

def box_access_C12() -> None:
    """30200-box_access."""
    global WS_ACCESS_REQUEST, WS_RENTER_VERIFIED
    if WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y': #30201
        verify_renter_C12() #30202
        if WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y': #30203
            log_access_C12() #30204
            escort_to_vault_C12() #30205

def verify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """30210-verify_renter."""
    global WS_RENTER_VERIFIED, BOX_RENTER, WS_BOX_NUMBER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_ID_VERIFIED, WS_KEY_VERIFIED
    WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N' #30210
    if BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_NUMBER -1] == WS_CUSTOMER_ID: #30211
        if WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y': #30212
            if WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y': #30213
                WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y' #30214

def log_access_C12() -> None:
    """30220-log_access."""
    global WS_ACCESS_LOG, WS_BOX_NUMBER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_PROCESS_DATE, ACCESS_LOG_RECORD
    WS_ACCESS_LOG = AccessLog()
    ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER = WS_BOX_NUMBER #30221
    ACCESS_CUSTOMER = WS_CUSTOMER_ID #30222
    ACCESS_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE #30223
    ACCESS_TIME = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S") #30224
    ACCESS_TYPE = 'ENTRY' #30225
    write_access_log_record(WS_ACCESS_LOG) #30226

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """30230-escort_to_vault."""
    global WS_DISPLAY_MSG
    WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED' #30230
    print(WS_DISPLAY_MSG) #30231

def box_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """30300-box_drilling."""
    global WS_DRILLING_REQUEST, WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED
    if WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y': #30301
        validate_drilling_auth_C12() #30302
        if WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y': #30303
            schedule_drilling_C12() #30304
            notify_renter_C12() #30305

def validate_drilling_auth_C12() -> None:
    """30310-validate_drilling_auth."""
    global WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED, WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS, WS_COURT_ORDER, WS_DECEASED_RENTER, WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED
    WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N' #30310
    if WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12: #30311
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y' #30312
    if WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y': #30313
        WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y' #30314
    if WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y': #30315
        if WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y': #30316
            WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y' #30317

def schedule_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """30320-schedule_drilling."""
    global WS_DRILLING_RECORD, WS_BOX_NUMBER, WS_DRILLING_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, DRILLING_RECORD
    WS_DRILLING_RECORD = DrillingRecord()
    DRILL_BOX_NUMBER = WS_BOX_NUMBER #30321
    DRILL_REASON = WS_DRILLING_REASON #30322
    DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE = int(WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30 #30323
    write_drilling_record(WS_DRILLING_RECORD) #30324

def notify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """30330-notify_renter."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'box_drilling' #30330
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL' #30331
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box' #30332
    send_notification_C12() #30333

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """30400-box_billing."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX, WS_TOTAL_BOXES, BOX_STATUS, BOX_RENEWAL_DUE
    WS_BOX_IDX = 1
    while WS_BOX_IDX <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES: #30401
        if BOX_STATUS[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'R': #30402
            if BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] == 'Y': #30403
                charge_annual_fee_C12() #30404
        WS_BOX_IDX += 1 #30401

def charge_annual_fee_C12() -> None:
    """30410-charge_annual_fee."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX, BOX_RENTER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, BOX_ANNUAL_FEE, WS_FEE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL
    WS_CUSTOMER_ID = BOX_RENTER[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] #30410
    WS_FEE_AMOUNT = BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] #30411
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_FEE_AMOUNT #30412
    update_account_C12() #30413
    BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] = BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX - 1] + 10000 #30414

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """31000-merchant_services."""
    process_authorization_C12() #31001
    capture_transaction_C12() #31002
    process_settlement_C12() #31003
    handle_chargeback_C12() #31004

def process_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """31100-process_authorization."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID, WS_FRAUD_APPROVED, WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE

    validate_card_C12() #31101
    if WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y': #31102
        check_fraud_score_C12() #31103
        if WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y': #31104
            check_available_credit_C12() #31105
            if WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y': #31106
                approve_auth_C12() #31107
            else:
                decline_auth_C12() #31108
        else:
            decline_auth_C12() #31109
    else:
        decline_auth_C12() #31110

def validate_card_C12() -> None:
    """31110-validate_card."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID, WS_LUHN_VALID, WS_NOT_EXPIRED, WS_CVV_VALID

    WS_CARD_VALID = 'N' #31110
    check_luhn_C12() #31111
    if WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y': #31112
        check_expiry_C12() #31113
        if WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y': #31114
            check_cvv_C12() #31115
            if WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y': #31116
                WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y' #31117

def check_luhn_C12() -> None:
    """31115-check_luhn."""
    global WS_LUHN_SUM, WS_LUHN_IDX, WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_LUHN_DIGIT, WS_LUHN_VALID

    WS_LUHN_SUM = 0 #31115
    WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
    while WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1: #31116
        WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[WS_LUHN_IDX - 1]) #31117
        if (17 - WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0: #31118
            WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2 #31119
            if WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9: #31120
                WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9 #31121
        WS_LUHN_SUM += WS_LUHN_DIGIT #31122
        WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1 #31116
    if WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0: #31123
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y' #31124
    else:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N' #31125

def check_expiry_C12() -> None:
    """31116-check_expiry."""
    global WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_NOT_EXPIRED

    if WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= WS_PROCESS_DATE: #31126
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y' #31127
    else:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N' #31128

def check_cvv_C12() -> None:
    """31117-check_cvv."""
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_CVV, WS_CVV_RESULT, WS_CVV_VALID

    cvvverify(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_CVV, WS_CVV_RESULT) #31129
    if WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M': #31130
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y' #31131
    else:
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'N' #31132

def check_fraud_score_C12() -> None:
    """31120-check_fraud_score."""
    global WS_AUTH_REQUEST, WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE, FRAUD_SCORE, WS_FRAUD_APPROVED, FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE

    fraudcheck(WS_AUTH_REQUEST, WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE) #31133
    if FRAUD_SCORE < 70: #31134
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y' #31135
    else:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N' #31136
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE #31137

def check_available_credit_C12() -> None:
    """31130-check_available_credit."""
    global WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC, CARD_ACCOUNT_FILE, WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE

    WS_SEARCH_KEY = WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER #31138
    WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = read_card_account_file(CARD_ACCOUNT_FILE, WS_SEARCH_KEY) #31139
    if WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= WS_AUTH_AMOUNT: #31140
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y' #31141
    else:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N' #31142
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51' #31143

def approve_auth_C12() -> None:
    """31140-approve_auth."""
    global WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT

    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00' #31144
    generate_auth_code_C12() #31145
    WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= WS_AUTH_AMOUNT #31146
    record_authorization_C12() #31147

def generate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """31145-generate_auth_code."""
    global WS_AUTH_CODE, WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE

    WS_AUTH_CODE = int(random_number() * 999999) #31148
    WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = WS_AUTH_CODE #31149

def record_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """31146-record_authorization."""
    global WS_AUTH_RECORD, WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_AMOUNT, WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_MERCHANT_ID, AUTH_RECORD
    WS_AUTH_RECORD = AuthRecord()
    AUTH_REC_CARD = WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER #31150
    AUTH_REC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTH_AMOUNT #31151
    AUTH_REC_CODE = WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE #31152
    AUTH_REC_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE #31153
    AUTH_REC_TIME = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S") #31154
    AUTH_REC_MERCHANT = WS_MERCHANT_ID #31155
    AUTH_REC_STATUS = 'P' #31156
    write_auth_record(WS_AUTH_RECORD) #31157

def move_ws_file_result_to_file_err_msg(ws_file_result: str, file_err_msg: str) -> str:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    file_err_msg = ws_file_result
    return file_err_msg

def move_function_current_date_to_file_err_timestamp(file_err_timestamp: datetime) -> datetime:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    file_err_timestamp = datetime.now()
    return file_err_timestamp

def write_file_error_record_from_ws_file_error_log(ws_file_error_log: str) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    # Assuming write_record is a function to write to a file
    write_record(ws_file_error_log)

def log_info_C13() -> None:
    """99810-log_info."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY, LOG_RECORD
    LOG_LEVEL = 'INFO'  # MOVE 'INFO' TO log_level
    LOG_MESSAGE = WS_LOG_MESSAGE  # MOVE ws_log_message TO log_message
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO log_timestamp
    LOG_RECORD = WS_LOG_ENTRY  # Assuming LOG_RECORD is a data structure
    write_log_record(LOG_RECORD)  # WRITE log_record FROM ws_log_entry.

def format_error_C13() -> None:
    """99910-format_error."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR
    WS_FORMATTED_ERROR = f"ERROR: {WS_ERROR_CODE} - {WS_ERROR_MSG}" # STRING 'ERROR: ' DELIMITED SIZE ...

def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """99920-display_error."""
    print(WS_FORMATTED_ERROR)  # DISPLAY ws_formatted_error.

def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """99930-write_error_log."""
    global WS_ERROR_LOG_REC, ERR_LOG_CODE, ERR_LOG_MSG, ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP, ERR_LOG_PROGRAM, ERR_LOG_PARAGRAPH, ERROR_LOG_RECORD
    WS_ERROR_LOG_REC = {} # INITIALIZE ws_error_log_rec (Assuming it's a dict)'
    ERR_LOG_CODE = WS_ERROR_CODE  # MOVE ws_error_code TO err_log_code
    ERR_LOG_MSG = WS_ERROR_MSG  # MOVE ws_error_msg TO err_log_msg
    ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO err_log_timestamp
    ERR_LOG_PROGRAM = WS_PROGRAM_NAME  # MOVE ws_program_name TO err_log_program
    ERR_LOG_PARAGRAPH = WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME  # MOVE ws_paragraph_name TO err_log_paragraph
    ERROR_LOG_RECORD = WS_ERROR_LOG_REC # MOVE ws_error_log_rec to ERROR_LOG_RECORD
    write_error_log_record(ERROR_LOG_RECORD)  # WRITE error_log_record FROM ws_error_log_rec.

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """32000-treasury_management."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """32100-calculate_cash_position."""
    global WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_CASH_POSITION = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_cash_position
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """32110-sum_vault_cash."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_VAULT_REC
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        try:
            WS_VAULT_REC = read_vault_cash_file() # READ vault_cash_file INTO ws_vault_rec
            VAULT_BALANCE = WS_VAULT_REC["VAULT_BALANCE"] #Assuming that WS_VAULT_REC is a dict containing VAULT_BALANCE key
            WS_CASH_POSITION += VAULT_BALANCE # ADD vault_balance TO ws_cash_position
        except StopIteration:  # AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag.

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """32120-sum_fed_account."""
    global WS_FED_BALANCE, WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_FED_BALANCE = read_fed_account_file()  # READ fed_account_file INTO ws_fed_balance
    WS_CASH_POSITION += WS_FED_BALANCE # ADD ws_fed_balance TO ws_cash_position.

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """32130-sum_correspondent_balances."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_CORR_REC
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        try:
            WS_CORR_REC = read_correspondent_file() # READ correspondent_file INTO ws_corr_rec
            CORR_BALANCE = WS_CORR_REC["CORR_BALANCE"] #Assuming that WS_CORR_REC is a dict containing CORR_BALANCE key
            WS_CASH_POSITION += CORR_BALANCE # ADD corr_balance TO ws_cash_position
        except StopIteration:  # AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag.

def update_cfp_document_C14() -> None:
    """33330-update_cfp_document.
       MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_cfp_update_date
       MOVE ws_cfp_status TO cfp_overall_status
       MOVE ws_available_funding TO cfp_total_sources
       MOVE ws_stressed_outflows TO cfp_stress_needs
       REWRITE cfp_record FROM ws_cfp_document."""
    global WS_CFP_STATUS, WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING, WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS
    WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    CFP_OVERALL_STATUS = WS_CFP_STATUS
    CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES = WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING
    CFP_STRESS_NEEDS = WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS
    # Assuming REWRITE means update a record in a file/database.  Need more info to implement.
    print(f"Updating CFP Record with: {WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE}, {CFP_OVERALL_STATUS}, {CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES}, {CFP_STRESS_NEEDS}")

def capital_management_C14() -> None:
    """34000-capital_management.
       PERFORM 34100-calculate_capital_ratios
       PERFORM 34200-risk_weighted_assets
       PERFORM 34300-capital_planning
       PERFORM 34400-stress_testing."""
    calculate_capital_ratios_C14()
    risk_weighted_assets_C14()
    capital_planning_C14()
    stress_testing_C14()

def calculate_capital_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """34100-calculate_capital_ratios.
       PERFORM 34110-calculate_tier1
       PERFORM 34120-calculate_tier2
       PERFORM 34130-calculate_ratios."""
    calculate_tier1_C14()
    calculate_tier2_C14()
    calculate_ratios_C14()

def calculate_tier1_C14() -> None:
    """34110-calculate_tier1.
       MOVE ZEROES TO ws_tier1_capital
       ADD ws_common_stock TO ws_tier1_capital
       ADD ws_retained_earnings TO ws_tier1_capital
       ADD ws_aoci TO ws_tier1_capital
       SUBTRACT ws_goodwill FROM ws_tier1_capital
       SUBTRACT ws_intangibles FROM ws_tier1_capital
       SUBTRACT ws_dta_deduction FROM ws_tier1_capital."""
    global WS_TIER1_CAPITAL, WS_COMMON_STOCK, WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS, WS_AOCI, WS_GOODWILL, WS_INTANGIBLES, WS_DTA_DEDUCTION
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_COMMON_STOCK
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_AOCI
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= WS_GOODWILL
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= WS_INTANGIBLES
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= WS_DTA_DEDUCTION

def calculate_tier2_C14() -> None:
    """34120-calculate_tier2.
       MOVE ZEROES TO ws_tier2_capital
       ADD ws_sub_debt TO ws_tier2_capital
       ADD ws_alll_eligible TO ws_tier2_capital
       COMPUTE ws_total_capital = 
          ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital."""
    global WS_TIER2_CAPITAL, WS_SUB_DEBT, WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL, WS_TIER1_CAPITAL
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += WS_SUB_DEBT
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + WS_TIER2_CAPITAL

def calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """34130-calculate_ratios.
       IF ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0
          COMPUTE ws_cet1_ratio = 
             (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
          COMPUTE ws_capital_ratio = 
             (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
       end_if
       IF ws_total_assets > 0
          COMPUTE ws_leverage_ratio = 
             (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100
       end_if."""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS, WS_CET1_RATIO, WS_TIER1_CAPITAL, WS_CAPITAL_RATIO, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL, WS_TOTAL_ASSETS, WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO
    if WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS > 0:
        WS_CET1_RATIO = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100
        WS_CAPITAL_RATIO = (WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100
    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:
        WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100

def risk_weighted_assets_C14() -> None:
    """34200-risk_weighted_assets.
       MOVE ZEROES TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
       PERFORM 34210-credit_rwa
       PERFORM 34220-market_rwa
       PERFORM 34230-operational_rwa."""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00")
    credit_rwa_C14()
    market_rwa_C14()
    operational_rwa_C14()

def credit_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """34210-credit_rwa.
       COMPUTE ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * 0.00
       COMPUTE ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * 0.00
       COMPUTE ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * 0.20
       COMPUTE ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * 0.50
       COMPUTE ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * 1.00
       COMPUTE ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * 1.00
       ADD ws_cash_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
       ADD ws_govt_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
       ADD ws_bank_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
       ADD ws_mortgage_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
       ADD ws_commercial_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
       ADD ws_consumer_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets."""
    global WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_GOVT_SECURITIES, WS_BANK_DEPOSITS, WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES, WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS, WS_CONSUMER_LOANS
    global WS_CASH_RWA, WS_GOVT_RWA, WS_BANK_RWA, WS_MORTGAGE_RWA, WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA, WS_CONSUMER_RWA, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS

    WS_CASH_RWA = WS_CASH_POSITION * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GOVT_RWA = WS_GOVT

def main_logic() -> None:
    """Main logic that calls other functions"""
    global WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL, WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL, WS_RECON_DIFF

    WS_RECON_DIFF = WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL - WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
    if WS_RECON_DIFF != Decimal("0.00"):
        log_recon_exception_C15()

def log_recon_exception_C15() -> None:
    """37235-log_recon_exception."""
    global WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_data, WS_GL_ACCOUNT, WS_RECON_DIFF, RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD

    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_data = WS_RECON_EXCEPTION()
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_data.RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT = WS_GL_ACCOUNT
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_data.RECON_EXC_DIFF = WS_RECON_DIFF
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_data.RECON_EXC_DATE = datetime.now().isoformat()
    RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD = str(WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_data)  # Assuming you need to convert the data class to a string for writing
    write_record("RECON_EXCEPTION_FILE", RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD)

def intercompany_recon_C15() -> None:
    """37300-intercompany_recon."""
    load_ic_balances_C15()
    match_ic_pairs_C15()
    report_ic_differences_C15()

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """37310-load_ic_balances."""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY, WS_IC_BALANCE_data
    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    intercompany_file_data = read_file(INTERCOMPANY_FILE)  # Read all lines from the file
    for line in intercompany_file_data:
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break
        try:
            # Assuming each line in the file can be directly mapped to WS_IC_BALANCE fields
            # Adjust the following line based on the actual structure of the file
            WS_IC_BALANCE_data = WS_IC_BALANCE(*line.split(','))  # Splitting the line into fields
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            if WS_IC_COUNT <= len(WS_IC_ARRAY):
                 WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = WS_IC_BALANCE_data
            else:
                print("Warning: WS_IC_ARRAY size exceeded.")

        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        except Exception as e:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            print(f"Error reading intercompany_file: {e}")
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """37320-match_ic_pairs."""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

def find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """37325-find_ic_counterpart."""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_IC_DIFF
    WS_SEARCH_FROM = IC_FROM_ENTITY(WS_IC_IDX)
    WS_SEARCH_TO = IC_TO_ENTITY(WS_IC_IDX)
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        if IC_FROM_ENTITY(WS_IC_IDX2) == WS_SEARCH_TO:
            if IC_TO_ENTITY(WS_IC_IDX2) == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                WS_IC_DIFF = IC_AMOUNT(WS_IC_IDX) + IC_AMOUNT(WS_IC_IDX2)
                if WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal("0.00"):
                    log_ic_diff_C15()
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

def log_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """37326-log_ic_diff."""
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC_data, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, IC_DIFF_RECORD
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_data = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_data.ICD_FROM = WS_SEARCH_FROM
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_data.ICD_TO = WS_SEARCH_TO
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_data.ICD_AMOUNT = WS_IC_DIFF
    IC_DIFF_RECORD = str(WS_IC_DIFF_REC_data)
    write_record("IC_DIFF_FILE", IC_DIFF_RECORD)

def load_nostro_statement_C15() -> None:
    """37410-load_nostro_statement."""
    global WS_NOSTRO_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_NOSTRO_ITEM_data

    WS_NOSTRO_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    nostro_statement_file_data = read_file(NOSTRO_STATEMENT_FILE)  # Read all lines

    for line in nostro_statement_file_data:
         if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
             break
         try:
            # Assuming each line maps to WS_NOSTRO_ITEM structure.  Adjust the following line based on your actual NOSTRO data
            WS_NOSTRO_ITEM_data = WS_NOSTRO_ITEM(*line.split(','))  # Splitting the line into fields
            WS_NOSTRO_COUNT += 1
         except EOFError:
             WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
         except Exception as e:
             WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
             print(f"Error reading nostro_statement_file: {e}")
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        move_to_archive_C15()
        compress_archive_C15()

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """38410-move_to_archive."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_RECORD_data, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, AUDIT_FILE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    audit_file_data = read_file(AUDIT_FILE)  # Read all lines from the AUDIT_FILE

    for line in audit_file_data:
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break

        # Assuming each line in the audit_file_data corresponds to WS_AUDIT_RECORD.
        WS_AUDIT_RECORD_data = WS_AUDIT_RECORD(*line.split(',')) # Adjust based on your actual record format

        if WS_AUDIT_RECORD_data.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
            ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD = str(WS_AUDIT_RECORD_data)
            write_record(ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD)
            delete_record(AUDIT_FILE)
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def compress_archive_C15() -> None:
    """38420-compress_archive."""
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

def performance_monitoring_C15() -> None:
    """39000-performance_monitoring."""
    collect_metrics_C15()
    analyze_performance_C15()
    generate_alerts_C15()
    optimize_resources_C15()

def collect_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """39100-collect_metrics."""
    cpu_metrics_C15()
    memory_metrics_C15()
    io_metrics_C15()
    transaction_metrics_C15()

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """39110-cpu_metrics."""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT
    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = call_program('GETCPU')
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'

def memory_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """39120-memory_metrics."""
    global WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION, WS_MEMORY_ALERT
    WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION = call_program('GETMEM')
    if WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION > 85:
        WS_MEMORY_ALERT = 'Y'

def io_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """39130-io_metrics."""
    global WS_IO_WAIT_TIME, WS_IO_THRESHOLD, WS_IO_ALERT
    WS_IO_WAIT_TIME = call_program('GETIO')
    if WS_IO_WAIT_TIME > WS_IO_THRESHOLD:
        WS_IO_ALERT = 'Y'

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """39140-transaction_metrics."""
    global WS_TPS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME
    WS_TPS = Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT) / Decimal(WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS)
    WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal(WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME) / Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT)

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """39200-analyze_performance."""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD, WS_PERF_DEGRADED, WS_TPS, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW
    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'
    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'

def generate_alerts_C15() -> None:
    """39300-generate_alerts."""
    global WS_CPU_ALERT, WS_MEMORY_ALERT, WS_PERF_DEGRADED
    if WS_CPU_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_cpu_alert_C15()
    if WS_MEMORY_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_memory_alert_C15()
    if WS_PERF_DEGRADED == 'Y':
        send_perf_alert_C15()

def send_cpu_alert_C15() -> None:
    """39310-send_cpu_alert."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CPU_UTILIZATION

    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'high_cpu'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'ALERT: CPU utilization at {WS_CPU_UTILIZATION}%'
    send_notification_C15()

def send_memory_alert_C15() -> None:

    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'high_memory'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'ALERT: High memory utilization'

def send_perf_alert_C15() -> None:

    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'PERFORMANCE'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'ALERT: Performance degradation detected'

def full_backup_C15() -> None:
    """40110-full_backup."""
    global WS_DAY_OF_WEEK, WS_BACKUP_STATUS, WS_LAST_FULL_BACKUP

    if WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7:
        WS_BACKUP_STATUS = call_program('FULLBKUP')
        if WS_BACKUP_STATUS == 'SUCCESS':
            WS_LAST_FULL_BACKUP = datetime.now().isoformat()

def incremental_backup_C15() -> None:
    """40120-incremental_backup."""
    global WS_BACKUP_STATUS, WS_LAST_INCR_BACKUP

    WS_BACKUP_STATUS = call_program('INCRBKUP')
    if WS_BACKUP_STATUS == 'SUCCESS':
        WS_LAST_INCR_BACK
